Apotheosis – Ascension to Godhood(new)
Vol.1

Chapter 2330
The upper-level true god struggled out of the severed tentacle and fled.
"Trying to escape?"
Seeing this, Luo Zheng was about to slash out again.
Although this thing was a ferocious creature in the abyss demon realm, it wasn't that powerful.
It wasn't hard to kill it.
Just as Luo Zheng was about to end it, Qian Long suddenly shouted, "Luo Zheng, don't kill it! This thing has intelligence! "
Although Patriarch Qian Long didn't express his meaning completely, Luo Zheng understood in an instant.
After leaving the land of reincarnation, they moved forward purely based on their intuition. They didn't know north, south, east, or west at all.
If they were lucky, they could leave this place and enter the upper levels of the abyss demon realm …
But if they were unlucky, they would enter the nest of some powerful ferocious creatures. That would be troublesome.
If this thing really had intelligence, they could get some information from it!
The huge triangular head spun crazily and quickly drilled out a huge hole. It wasn't slow at all.
However, its size ultimately affected its speed.
Luo Zheng's figure floated lightly and arrived behind the "head". He grabbed the tentacles behind it and pulled it out. The "head" was pulled out by Luo Zheng.
"Bang!"
Then, he kicked the area below his head.
The huge head drew a parabola and landed on the beach.
"Waaa …"
The huge mouth let out an unpleasant shriek. After rolling around, it started to spin again.
It still didn't give up on escaping.
However, Qian Long had already landed on the beach. He lightly stepped on the sand and a surge of Taoist Connotation entered the sand.
This piece of sand suddenly became hard and hard, like a stone slab, its surface was suffused with a multicolored color.
The head tried to drill for a while, but there was no way it could go down. In the end, it could only give up.
When Luo Zheng landed next to Qian Long, Qian Long stared at the head and said, "If I'm not wrong, this is the West Sea Devil Head."
"What's the West Sea Devil Head Monster?" Luo Zheng had never seen such a strange thing before.
"It's an ancient legend in the Western Sea. This kind of monster floats in the depths of the Western Sea. If someone accidentally gets caught by its tentacles, they'll be pulled to the bottom of the sea," said Qianlong.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng understood. He smiled and asked, "It seems that this is just a legend, and not many people know about it."
In the first place, the Abyssal Magic Domain did not exist. Everything in it was a fantasy created by the gods.
Perhaps because of that ancient legend, the West Sea Demon Head Monster was passed down by word of mouth. In people's imaginations, it was still born in the Abyss Demonic Realm.
"Indeed." Patriarch Qianlong nodded. "The legend of the West Sea Devil Head is only spread around the West Sea. I was born there, so I naturally know."
There weren't many legends about the West Sea Demon Head Monster, and very few people knew about it. The power of faith it received was also very little, so it was only natural that it became weaker …
But this thing was said to be very cunning and intelligent. Patriarch Qianlong thought of this and asked Luo Zheng to spare its life.
Luo Zheng tapped his toes lightly and jumped up, standing above the Demon Head Monster. "Which layer of the Abyss Demonic Realm is this? Where is the exit?"
He didn't want to talk too much nonsense with this ugly thing.
"Wah wah wah …"
The head opened its mouth and made a series of strange sounds, as if it didn't understand human language.
Luo Zheng's expression didn't change at all. He pointed the long sword in his hand downward. "Speak human language, or die immediately."
"Crash …"
Billowing sword intent circled around Luo Zheng's long sword. He only needed to shake his hand slightly to mince the head into pieces.
The Demon Head Monster felt Luo Zheng's killing intent and finally opened its mouth, shouting in a very rough and strange voice, "Don't, don't kill me, I'll tell you!"
Luo Zheng and Patriarch Qianlong looked at each other and smiled. Patriarch Qianlong's judgment was right.
"Speak," Luo Zheng said lightly.
"I don't know much … I'm just their food. This is a breeding ground!" The Demon Head Monster shouted.
"Food? A breeding ground? "Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed slightly.
"Yes, we're just food for the demons! Every once in a while, those demons will come here, dig us out, and take us away. "The Demon Head Monster's face showed helplessness.
The Demon Clan's control of the Abyss Demonic Realm had reached the extreme. Relying on their own wisdom, it could be said that they had surpassed the definition of "Forbidden Lands".
This was also why the Demon Clan could fight against the Saints!
This breeding ground was just a ranch for the Demon Clan. Whether it was in the magma lake or the endless beach, there were delicious food kept.
And the Demon Head Monster was one of them.
"Which layer of the Abyss Demonic Realm is this?" Patriarch Qianlong suddenly asked. This was what he was most concerned about.
"The sixth layer," the Demon Head Monster answered honestly.
Hearing this answer, the hearts of the six people present sank.
Although they did not know how many layers there were in the Abyss Demonic Realm, this answer was far beyond their expectations!
"The sixth layer, it seems like we can't get out …" Patriarch Qianlong murmured with a bitter smile on his face.
He even suspected that the sixth layer was the lowest layer of the Abyss Demonic Realm!
"If we really can't leave, we should return to the Land of Reincarnation."
"If we encounter the legendary demons, we won't have a chance to leave alive …"
The three upper true gods were unable to remain calm.
Luo Zheng frowned, stared at the Demon Head Monster, and asked, "Is there a way to return to the fifth layer?"
"I … don't know." The Demon Head Monster's huge face was full of tears. "I really don't know. I've stayed in this damned place for long, but I can't go anywhere. In the end, I'll only become food for the demons!"
Compared to the Land of the Gods, the environment in the Forbidden Lands was even worse.
These monsters would fight with each other, and there was no way for them to escape.
After all, it was impossible for them to escape into the Land of the Gods. The creatures in the Forbidden Lands were just imaginary creatures. If they left the Forbidden Lands, they would really disappear.
"In that case, there's no need for you to exist." As Luo Zheng spoke, he raised the long sword in his hand and prepared to kill the Demon Head Monster.
These monsters did not have the slightest value of mercy.
"Wait, wait." The Demon Head Monster saw that it could not escape death and suddenly shouted, "I know, there's a guy in the sea. He should know the way out!"
"In the magma sea?"

Chapter 2331
The monster hesitated for a moment before saying, "That thing lives in the magma and is one of the demons' favorite foods. But that thing is too agile, so I usually use my body to fish it up …"
"Use your body?" Luo Zheng blinked and swept his gaze across the beach.
"Yes," the monster answered truthfully, its "human face" looking rather aggrieved.
The monster was considered a relatively low-level monster in this area.
It might be one of the demons' food, but its greatest use was to lure that thing in the sea …
"In that case, we can also fish it up!" Patriarch Qianlong said.
"I'm afraid you'll have to wait …" the monster suddenly said.
"Why should we wait?" Luo Zheng asked with a frown.
"That guy was eaten by the demons not long ago. I don't know if it has been reborn or not." The monster laughed mischievously.
All the ferocious creatures in the forbidden area would appear again and again.
Just like shenwu coins, after taking shenwu coins from the same place, a similar amount of shenwu coins would appear again after a period of time. This was because the power of faith was constantly supporting the forbidden area.
In contrast, the monster's luck might be better than the guy in the magma sea.
After all, monsters were not so popular with the demons. As long as the guy in the magma sea was "reborn", it would be caught by the demons and eaten again.
Who knew how many times this absurd cycle had repeated.
Hearing the monster's words, Luo Zheng said, "Let's assume that guy has been reborn. I can also give it a try!"
Then, Luo Zheng reached out and pulled in the air.
One of the tentacles buried deep under the beach was pulled out by Luo Zheng.
After grabbing the tentacle tightly, he shook it forcefully. The tentacle drew an arc in the air and fell into the surface of the magma sea.
Although the temperature of the magma sea was not low, the tentacle could withstand it.
Not long after, the tentacle sank into the sea of magma …
Seeing this, Patriarch Qianlong smiled and also pulled out a tentacle from the beach.
Like Luo Zheng, he threw the tentacle into the magma sea.
The three upper true gods slowly descended and waited motionlessly.
However, they were still somewhat afraid of the Devil Head Monster. They only floated to the side, not daring to land on the beach.
"You guys … take your time fishing. Can I leave first?" The demon asked.
"What do you think?" Luo Zheng glanced at the monster.
The monster shut its mouth and didn't make a sound.
Although the Devil Head Monster was a ferocious creature in the Forbidden Grounds and could be reborn infinitely, the Devil Head Monster could still be reborn.
However, every time it died, its cultivation base would decrease.
The cultivation of forbidden lands and vicious creatures was different from the Divine Realm's system. Their cultivation was measured by the power of faith they contained.
After dying once, the power of faith that he had accumulated would be completely wasted, and he would have to start all over again.
Therefore, the beasts in this area all had miserable fates. They could only be reborn over and over again, living eternally at the bottom of the food chain.
Only a small number of ferocious beasts were able to climb up.
Of course, even those ferocious creatures that climbed up could only be cannon fodder for the demons.
After all, demons stood at the top of the entire Abyssal Demon Domain.
The two tentacles were immersed in the magma silently.
Luo Zheng grabbed one of them tightly and stood on the beach like a statue.
Taking advantage of this free time, Luo Zheng used the reincarnation soul tool to read Hua Tianming's flame of memory again.
"As expected of a group of Saints. After rushing out of the purple fog, they quickly subdued those little demons … So soon, they're going to step into the fourth level?" Luo Zheng said silently in his heart.
Dongfang Chunjun led the Saints from bottom to top. After rushing out of the purple fog, those little demons still didn't give up.
However, as the Soul Devouring Worms dispersed, the Saints could also release their divine consciousness freely.
This was like a blind man suddenly opening his eyes to see the world. After using his divine consciousness to lock onto the position of the little demons, the speed advantage of these little demons disappeared.
Dongfang Chunjun didn't need to do anything.
Even those quasi-sages, or even a few top-notch Great Perfection True Gods, would have the power to fight against these little devils!
After two of the little demons were captured alive, Tang Lun even created a fire formation and placed these two little demons in the formation to barbecue them. In the end, the two little demons were completely burned.
The other five little demons also knew that they couldn't fight against these people. They could only hide in the purple fog and retreat with the Soul Devouring Worms.
Although the little demons could be reborn infinitely, they wouldn't foolishly send themselves to death.
After that, the people from the Wealthy Family Alliance were like a hot knife through butter. They didn't encounter any resistance on the third level of the Abyssal Demon Domain and quickly rushed to the entrance of the fourth level of the Abyssal Demon Domain.
If the thick fog above the Abyssal Demon Domain was completely dispersed, one would discover that the entire Abyssal Demon Domain was like a huge terrace.
From the outermost periphery, the center of this huge pit was the lowest level of the Abyssal Demon Domain.
Around a huge altar at the lowest level, jade-white skulls were placed neatly.
These skulls had circles of special patterns drawn on them, which looked very artistic.
This was the craftsmanship of demons.
The skulls of true gods were very valuable in the demon clan.
It wasn't that these skulls had any practical use for the demon clan. The demons only treated these skulls as treasures and hung them in their bedrooms for them to admire.
"Huala, huala …"
In the center of this altar, there was a small circular hole.
The small hole suddenly began to spurt out blood. As the blood spread, it flowed along the patterns on the altar and poured into the skulls around the altar.
After the skulls were filled with blood, their two sunken eye sockets began to emit a dark red light.
When the eye sockets of all the skulls began to emit light …
A pool of blood began to rise from the center of the altar.
This pool of blood quickly condensed and turned into the shape of a human baby.
It began to grow from the size of a thumb to the size of a fist. Suddenly, two small horns grew out of its head and its skin turned dark red. It was the appearance of the little demon from before.
"Damned Sage, how dare he refine me! They are seeking their own deaths! "
That little demon still hadn't completely "grown up". Its slightly immature face was already filled with rage.
But just as it finished speaking, a huge fist suddenly descended from the top of the altar.
"Boom!"
This fist directly smashed onto the altar. Under the pressure of the fist, the little demon was completely turned into a meat patty.

Chapter 2332
Above the altar, in the thick fog, a huge figure stood in the middle.
When the figure slowly retracted its fist, the little fiend's originally flat body twisted and slowly returned to its original appearance.
"Master …"
The little fiend knelt on the altar and called out in fear and trepidation.
Next to the little fiend, another pool of blood appeared. This pool of blood quickly condensed and turned into another little fiend.
After these two little fiends were captured by the Saints, they were refined to death by Tang Lun. Therefore, the two of them were directly reborn at the bottom of the Abyss Demon Domain, saving them the travel time.
Of course, they were not willing to return early.
This mission was a complete failure, and whoever returned first would have to bear the master's anger.
"Master, you can't blame us. Wrath came too late. The Soul-Devouring Worms have already begun to dissipate. By the time the Saints rushed out of the area covered by the Soul-Devouring Worms, we were no match for them …"
"Boom!"
The other little fiend explained for a long time, but what greeted it was still a huge fist.
This time, the two little fiends were smashed into meat patties together.
However, this kind of punishment was not considered punishment at all to the little fiends.
They did not feel pain, and had no concept of physical pain.
The two little fiends were afraid of other punishments from their master. For the fiend race, torture methods could be said to be extremely plentiful. They had already raised some special torture to the height of art!
In fact, if one did not talk about their ugly appearance, the fiend race did indeed have excellent artistic talent.
"Forget it, it's just a test. The other party is a group of top forces in the Divine Domain. It's impossible to stop them with just you guys," the huge voice in the fog said faintly.
"This is an impossible mission, and they assigned it to us …" One of the little fiends muttered.
These little fiends were originally created from the negative emotions of the fiend race. Their temperament was extremely bad, and naturally could not control their mouths.
"Do you want to become food for the Demonic Hounds?" The huge voice in the fog shouted coldly.
That little fiend immediately shut up …
"Boom, boom, boom …"
With the sound of huge footsteps, the huge figure walked through the fog.
Following the altar to the right, there was a road that was hundreds of feet wide.
The road was flat to the extreme, and the square stones used to pave the road were tightly pressed together.
The surface of each square stone was engraved with extremely complicated patterns that connected with each other, leaving a beautiful picture on the surface of the road.
It was hard to imagine that the demons who represented chaos would decorate their settlement to such an extent.
At the end of the path was a huge hall that was thousands of feet tall. The hall was supported by sixty-six stone pillars, and each pillar was carved with a different shape.
In the end, the Abyss Demonic Realm could not be compared to the Divine Realm. It did not have such abundant resources, and the materials used to build the hall were definitely not as good as the floating islands of the wealthy families.
However, judging from its appearance, this giant hall was more exquisite and grandiose than any of the True Gods' buildings.
The huge figure followed the path all the way to the entrance of the giant hall. The mist that shrouded his body slowly dispersed, revealing his true body.
It was a six thousand feet tall Great Evil Demon.
This Great Evil Demon's face was ferocious, and the skin all over his body was dark red. His huge tendons contained a natural explosive power.
In fact, the imagination of the God's people and the power of faith from generation to generation had long imagined the demons as a kind of vicious creature that was perfect and extremely destructive.
All kinds of shiny pendants hung on this Great Evil Demon's body. There were hexagons, triangles, and extremely complicated geometric patterns.
"Boom!"
He walked to the entrance of the giant hall and pushed the door open. He then said indifferently, "Jia Lu, those fellows have already reached the fourth floor!"
In the giant hall, there was a huge round table.
There were three other Great Evil Demons sitting on the round table. At this moment, these three Great Evil Demons were eating with their heads lowered.
On the round table, there was a kind of creature that was as tall as a person.
These creatures had three legs and two hands. They were very fast, running rapidly on the huge round table, carrying food back and forth.
The three-legged, two-handed vicious creatures were called Demonic Spirits. They were also creatures created by the demons, but their duty was only to serve these Great Evil Demons.
"Kacha!"
A Great Demon in front of the round table was stuffing bright red berries into his mouth one after another.
These berries came from the "Enchanted Tree" in the Abyss. It was also a kind of fruit that the demons loved very much. The freshly picked enchanted berries emitted a fishy sweet smell of fresh blood.
After Jia Lu chewed on the berries, a thick smell of fresh blood spread in the hall. Some of the Demonic Spirits glanced at it and revealed a greedy look. Then, they diligently served the Great Evil Demons.
"Are we afraid of them? Tut? " After Jia Lu finished eating those berries, he carefully wiped his thick and sharp fingers as he casually asked in return.
"Of course not," Tut replied.
Jia Lu cracked open his mouth and smiled faintly. "Then what are you worried about?"
The vicious creatures in the Abyss could be reborn infinitely, and the demons were no exception.
However, every time they died, they would lose the power of faith they had accumulated, and their strength would be weakened.
However, the status of the demon race was stable, and this little impact was minimal.
"I think this is an opportunity. We can't be confined to the Abyss Demon Territory forever." Tut's face revealed a yearning look. "We've thought about it for so many years, and finally there's a glimmer of possibility. Why not use them to try? Although it's a little risky … "
The vicious creatures in the Forbidden Lands were born strong, and this was where they were lucky.
However, they also had the greatest misfortune. Whether it was a strong Great Evil Demon or a weak Ground Fox in the Forbidden Lands, they were not real. They were still fantasy creatures!
After these Great Evil Demons became the top existence in the Abyss Demon Territory, they began to think about how to leave this damned place!
Their goal was the entire Divine Territory.
However, as a fantasy creature, they would disappear once they left the Forbidden Lands. This was an iron law, and the demons could not avoid it.
However, these demons with extraordinary intelligence still found a glimmer of possibility. This possibility was enough for them to leave the Forbidden Lands and enter the Divine Territory.
"Hahaha …" Jia Lu suddenly laughed.
The other two Great Evil Demons were laughing too.
Tut frowned and asked, "What are you laughing about?"

Chapter 2333
There were not many Evil Demons, there were less than a thousand of them in the entire Abyss Demon Region.
The four Great Evil Demons in the hall were the actual commanders of the Evil Demons.
If there was an important decision, the four Great Evil Demons would usually vote.
"Of course I won't support you," Tut said with a serious expression.
He looked over Jia Lu's head and looked at the huge mural behind him.
On the mural was an old man holding a book, and around this God, there were countless Evil Demons.
Below the Evil Demons were countless True Gods in the Divine Domain.
Like the Gods, the Evil Demons also had a target of faith.
And the target of their faith was an ordinary old man in the Divine Domain.
This old man was called "Wang Xi", until his lifespan was almost up, Wang Xi was only a Proof of Divinity martial artist.
Because without Proof of Divinity, it was impossible to live forever in the Divine Domain.
An ordinary God's citizen like this was just an insignificant existence in the God's Domain.
If it was not for Wang Xi's senseless actions, it would not have had such a huge impact … …
Wang Xi's talent in cultivation was average.
Becoming a Proof of Divinity martial artist was his limit, he could no longer cultivate to become a Lower True God.
As time passed, Wang Xi's lifespan gradually came to an end.
But in his final years, he began to make up all kinds of legends.
In the era Wang Xi was in, the Sacred Clan had just disappeared, the newly born Gods in the Divine Domain were still in a state of sleep.
The legends he made up were widely spread … …
And among these legends, the most widely spread was the legend about the Evil Demons.
This race did not have a specific surname, so they had strange names, such as Jia Lu, Tut, Tut … …
This race was extremely intelligent … …
This race was strong and loved to kill … …
They lived in the Abyss Demon Region, where all the evil existences in the Divine Domain gathered.
Because of Wang Xi's nonsense, and by chance, after being spread by countless Gods by word of mouth, the Abyss Demon Region was born.
From this point of view, Wang Xi did indeed create the Evil Demons.
So in the eyes of the Evil Demons, Wang Xi's position was naturally extraordinary, even though he was only a Proof of Divinity martial artist.
"Alright, since the three of you insist, I won't object anymore." Jia Lu smiled and used his huge fingers to snap lightly.
Three Demonic Spirits carried a huge silver dinner plate and placed it gently in front of Jia Lu.
"I'll let you enjoy the delicacy this time, Tut...." Lu pushed the dinner plate to the other side of the round table.
"Dong dong dong … …"
There was a huge lid on top of the silver plate. The lid was constantly trembling, as if there was a living creature struggling crazily inside.
Tutt smiled and walked to the front of the round table.
Demonic Tut Tut of Divinity of Divinity of Divinity of Divinity of Divinity of Divinity of Divinity of Divinity of Divinity.
Tut Tut Divinity Divinity Divinity Divinity Divinity of Divinity....
"Damned demons, you'll all go extinct one day!"
"All of you have been hiding in the darkness of the Demon Domain all your lives, never seeing the light of day, yet you still think that you are strong …"
"I …"
The strange fish kept cursing.
Tutt was not angry at all.
The Demon Clan had already eaten this strange fish hundreds of thousands of times. This strange fish did not have the strength to choose the Demon Clan, so every time it was eaten, it would use all sorts of malicious words.
For the Demon Clan, they had already heard so much that their ears had grown calluses.
But no matter how vicious the strange fish was, it couldn't hide its delicious taste …
Tut picked up the silver wine cup and poured the magma in the cup onto the plate.
"Buzz buzz …"
The strange fish immediately opened up a transparent barrier, blocking the magma outside.
Tute extended a little finger and gently tapped on the barrier.
"Boom!"
Like a soap bubble, the barrier was instantly extinguished.
The boiling lava wrapped around the strange fish, making a sizzling sound. Amidst a series of miserable screams, the strange fish was cooked.
Tut took a whiff of the fragrance and said in an intoxicated manner, "When I'm enjoying this, something happy should appear!"
Another Great Demon gently clapped his hands …
A dozen or so human females flew out from the depths of the palace!
These human females had fair skin and beautiful looks. They only had a translucent veil draped over their bodies.
Accompanied by a burst of beautiful music, these women began to dance.
These human females all had the cultivation of an upper True God.
They were all descendants of the Floating Island's wealthy families. They were captured by the Demon Clan while exploring the Abyss Devil Domain.
The descendants of the wealthy families of the past had no choice but to fall to such a state …
After going through rounds of torture, the humiliation in their hearts had long disappeared. What replaced it was numbness.
With this wonderful music and dance, the Great Demons began their delightful banquet.
…
…
After the strange fish was eaten by Tut, it was reborn in the Abyss Devil Domain's magma sea.
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
The moment it was born, the strange fish had already opened up a barrier.
"Damned Wang Xi, creating these Demons that are worse than pigs and dogs!" the strange fish cursed viciously.
Ferocious creatures that could be born in the Abyss Devil Domain were not good creatures. After all, this place gathered the dark side of all the Gods …
The strange fish had been repeatedly eaten by the Demon Clan. It had heard many secrets about the Demon Clan at the dining table. It understood the Demon Clan rather well, better than any other creature!
After opening up the barrier, the strange fish swam around in the magma sea continuously.
Vaguely, it saw another delicious tentacle …
"So soon, they want to fish me out again! Are these pigs and dogs that hungry? "
The strange fish revealed a strange expression.
Logically speaking, every time it was eaten, it would be released for a period of time. The Demon Clan would not frequently fish it out in a short period of time.
As a unique Ferocious Spirit, it was an extremely high-end food among the Demon Clan. It was not something that ordinary Demons could enjoy …
"This shouldn't be …"
"I'm going to be eaten again in a short period of time?"
"I'm not that stupid!"
The strange fish resisted that tentacle.
However, in the strange fish's eyes, the Demon Head Demon's tentacle was very tempting. It was as tempting as the strange fish was to the Demon Clan.
It was going to be eaten anyway. Before it was eaten, it could still have a good meal!
"I don't care anymore!"
After an intense mental struggle, the strange fish finally bit the tentacle.
At the same time, Luo Zheng, who was on the beach, felt his tentacle suddenly sink. He smiled. "It seems … it took the bait!"

Chapter 2334
"Splash …"
The strange fish that was biting the tentacle was also dragged out of the sea of magma and fell toward the shore.
"That's not right …"
The strange fish's brain couldn't wrap itself around it.
According to the usual practice, it should have at least eaten most of the tentacle before it was dragged ashore!
If the demons wanted to catch it, it couldn't escape.
Therefore, the strange fish generally chose to give up resistance and eat first.
The demons that came to fish were also very patient and would let it enjoy a "last lunch."
But this time, he had only taken a bite before he was dragged up. Just how impatient were these guys!
"So fast?"
The demon's face also revealed a look of surprise.
Patriarch Qian Long smiled slightly and threw away the tentacle in his hand. It seemed that Luo Zheng's luck was indeed stronger than his.
"Plop!"
The strange fish directly smashed onto the beach. Its strange fish eyes looked around and the first thing it saw was the demon.
"Ha, demon, you dare to climb out of the sand with such a timid courage …"
Halfway through its words, the strange fish had already discovered that something was wrong.
Beside the demon, there were actually a few humans.
"Human, human True Gods? How could they appear here! "The strange fish shouted, its fish eyes filled with disbelief.
This was the core area of the demon race.
The only possibility for humans to appear here was that they were kept by the demon race … Then, the demons must want to eat humans!
In the strange fish's memory, the demons had done such a thing.
Perhaps the human race was a race comparable to the demon race, so the demon race was quite interested in everything about the human race.
Human flesh was naturally one of them.
But it was too difficult to eat humans …
After all, although this strange fish was rare and there was only one of it, it would be reborn after eating it.
It had been eaten hundreds of thousands of times, so there were hundreds of thousands of strange fish, and it was inexhaustible.
As for humans …
They could only go to the upper level of the Abyss Demon Realm to catch them.
The True Gods who dared to step into the Abyss Demon Realm were not good people. Even if they were caught, there wouldn't be too many of them.
Therefore, the demons discussed and decided to keep the humans here.
Let the true gods of the human race reproduce on their own. Once there were enough of them, they could eat at will.
Although this idea was beautiful, the True Gods who entered the Abyss Demon Region were all descendants of wealthy families!
Just how ambitious were these True Gods? How could they be willing to be raised like animals?
They would rather commit suicide than allow their children to be eaten by the Demon Clan.
In the end, the Demon Race could only give up. At most, they raised some human women to serve these Great Demons.
Even the female humans were given good treatment by the Demon Clan. They even gave them pointers in their cultivation. This gave them hope that one day, the powerhouses of the God Realm would slaughter their way into the Abyss Devil Domain and rescue them.
Even so, there were still some human women who took action at critical moments and chose to self-detonate after turning the world upside down …
Now that the strange fish saw a human True God here, its eyeballs were about to fall out of their sockets.
"I don't know either. You can ask them!" The devil said helplessly.
It and the strange fish were brothers in the same boat. The only difference was that the devil was not as popular as the strange fish. It did not know if this was a blessing or a curse.
The strange fish stared at Luo Zheng for a while and made a request. "Can I eat this devil? I can only eat its tentacles every time. I'm a little sick of it. I want to eat its head. "
"You!" The devil glared at him.
Luo Zheng smiled and said, "I want to know how to leave this place. The devil said you are more familiar with this place."
"Leave this place?" The strange fish stared at Luo Zheng as if it did not understand.
"Yes." Luo Zheng nodded.
"That's impossible." The strange fish chuckled. "Although I don't know how you came in, there's no way you can return from the sixth level!"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng and Patriarch Qian Long's faces darkened at the same time. Luo Zheng immediately said, "Speak clearly."
The strange fish looked at the devil again and said, "I'll only tell you if I eat this guy!"
"Then let me eat you first!" The Devil Head Monster wasn't easy to endure. It angrily rolled its huge head over.
"Bang!"
The devil's head had just rolled when it was kicked out by Luo Zheng and landed on the beach hundreds of feet away.
Then, Luo Zheng stretched out his hand and waved gently.
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh …"

The strange fish smiled bitterly. "But I can't leave the Abyss Demon Realm. How are you going to take me away?"
Like the devil, the strange fish was also a fantasy creature. It would disappear after leaving the forbidden area.
"The Abyss Demon Realm is so huge. You can hide in a corner and escape your current fate," Luo Zheng said.
Although the devil race was the top existence in the Abyss Demon Realm, they were not the ruler of the Abyss Demon Realm. The ruler of the forbidden area could only be a True God, not even a Sage.
The strange fish's eyes looked around, and it seemed to be tempted.
It did have such an idea. Hiding was better than being eaten over and over again …
But with its ability, it was not enough to escape!

Taking a step back, even if it failed, it would be nothing more than being eaten by the demon again. Its fate was already tragic to the extreme, and it couldn't possibly be worse.
"Okay! I'll take you to find a way out! "After thinking for a while, the strange fish did not hesitate and directly agreed.

But while it was eating, it still looked at the devil in the distance …
The devil knew that this guy would not give up on eating it, so it turned its head and went down the beach. This time, Luo Zheng did not stop it. Anyway, he had achieved his goal. Now, the key was up to this strange fish.

"A way to leave the Abyss Demon Realm?" Luo Zheng was surprised.

Chapter 2335

At this point, the strange fish's eyes revealed a yearning look.
As long as it was an intelligent creature, it probably wouldn't be willing to curl up in this damned place.

This strange fish was extremely unwilling to accept its fate of being repeatedly devoured. It had also plotted to escape several times.

Whether it was culture or art, the Fiend Clan was extremely particular about it.
This kind of attention was paid to all aspects, including food.
The Fiends were unable to dabble in things outside the Divine Realm, so naturally, everything in the Abyss Demon Domain was displayed to the extreme.

Chapter 2336

Chapter 2339

Luo Zheng and the others felt Clan Leader Qianlong's divine sense and naturally understood his actions. They all looked at him.
"This area is very spacious. Everyone restrain your aura and move forward carefully," Clan Leader Qianlong said.
Luo Zheng and the others nodded and retracted their aura to the extreme. They glided silently along the ground. This area was filled with lava rivers, and the dark-red lava reflected on their faces, making them look bright red.
Just as Clan Leader Qianlong said, there was nothing abnormal in this area, and there were no ferocious creatures.
Luo Zheng used the reincarnation soul tool to connect to the place of reincarnation. He wanted to observe the movements of Hua Tianming and the others.
The stone mouse said that Yue Yinliu would definitely return to the place of reincarnation. According to this, Emperor Hanqing was very likely to use this opportunity to return.
If Emperor Hanqing led Dongfang Chunjun here, Luo Zheng was afraid that he would really be in trouble.
He had absolutely no chance of winning against a large group of Sages alone. This was also one of the reasons Luo Zheng left.
Otherwise, he would have taken more time and patience to read the flame of memory.
After all, Luo Zheng still had many mysteries in his heart that had not been solved.
Fortunately, Luo Zheng now had the reincarnation soul tool. Under the situation of controlling the place of reincarnation, even if he left here, he could still read the flame of memory.
"Hu hu hu hu hu hu …"
In an hour, the six of them had already traveled several hundred kilometers without making a sound.
Along the way, there were more and more lava rivers below. When these lava rivers converged, they formed an even wider sea of lava!
At the edge of the lava sea was a pale green beach. The beach was covered with a kind of green and lustrous sand.
Everyone rested on the beach and discussed for a while. In the end, they decided to cross the magma sea directly.
However, just as they made their decision, tribe leader Qian Long's expression changed slightly. He turned his head and looked over.
When Luo Zheng saw Patriarch Qianlong's expression, he revealed a trace of confusion. He released his Divine Sense and swept along the edge of the magma sea.
On this infinitely long coastline, Luo Zheng didn't notice any other living creatures.
Ever since they walked out of the Land of Reincarnation, they realized that the Abyss Demon Realm did not live up to its name.
He originally thought that the Abyss Demon Domain should be filled with all kinds of cruel and fierce monsters, but it was empty everywhere …
"What's wrong?" Luo Zheng asked.
Patriarch Qianlong also revealed a trace of confusion. "Something on the beach is spying on us!"
"What is it!"
"Where is it?"
The faces of the three upper true gods immediately revealed nervous expressions.
"I didn't find anything unusual." Luo Zheng shook his head and said.
"That feeling was just a flash." Patriarch Qianlong thought for a moment and then said, "Forget it, we'd better hurry."
In Patriarch Qianlong's eyes, the most important thing now was to determine their position. After confirming their position, they would leave layer by layer.
However, the Abyss Demon Realm spanned across several large regions. It was really too huge …
Just as they were about to set off again, the sand under one of the upper True Gods' feet began to squirm.
The sand spiraled and crazily spun, forming a powerful suction force that sucked the Upper True God down!
Luo Zheng's reaction speed was extremely fast. He reached out and grabbed the upper True God's shoulder.
"Kacha!"
The suction force was beyond Luo Zheng's expectations. Luo Zheng only tore a piece of cloth from the upper True God's shoulder, and then the upper True God was pulled down into the ground.
"Below!"
"Leave quickly!"
When the other two upper True Gods saw this, they were alarmed and flew up into the air one after another.
However, Luo Zheng and Patriarch Qianlong still maintained their calm.
With their strength, as long as they did not encounter those Abyss Demons, they would not be in any particular danger.
Luo Zheng and Patriarch Qianlong looked at each other, and Patriarch Qianlong took the lead.
With a wave of his hands, he abruptly lifted them downward!
"Whoosh!"
The green sand rolled to the sides after being affected by an invisible force.
A thousand-foot-deep gully suddenly appeared on the beach!
On both sides of the gully, green tentacles were constantly squirming, and the upper True God was wrapped in one of the tentacles.
From the looks of it, there seemed to be a huge octopus hiding under the beach.
"Whoosh!"
Luo Zheng did not hesitate in the slightest, already flying towards that upper true god.
On both sides of the gully, the thick tentacles swept toward Luo Zheng! ·
"Break for me!"
Without waiting for Luo Zheng to act, Patriarch Qianlong above took out a reddish-gold whip.
He suddenly brandished the long whip, and from the end of the long whip, a series of five-colored whip shadows extended!
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
Patriarch Qianlong had immersed himself in the Five Elements Divine Dao for many years and had long since reached the acme of perfection in this Dao. He had even comprehended three types of Outer Dao Divine Abilities.
When he attacked like this, the power was naturally not to be underestimated.
The chaotic whip shadows almost enveloped Luo Zheng. Before the tentacles could touch Luo Zheng, they were cut off by the whip shadows!
However, the tentacles wrapped around the upper True God were rapidly shrinking.
Before Luo Zheng could arrive, the upper True God was pulled deeper into the sand …
Luo Zheng frowned and punched the sand on one side of the gully!
"Whoosh!"
Under the surge of a huge amount of power, a huge amount of sand was lifted, and the entire beach rolled in waves. The surface of the beach was completely removed by Luo Zheng.
The vicious creature hidden on the beach finally revealed its true appearance!
The entire beach was covered with thousands of green tentacles, and the source of these tentacles was connected to a huge head.
This head was triangular in shape and had a human face. It looked repulsive.
At this moment, it was using one of its tentacles to wrap around the upper True God and stuffed him into its bloody mouth.
When the upper True God smelled the fishy smell, despair filled his face.
At this moment, a sword light suddenly flashed …
"Crack!"
The thousands of tentacles connected to the head were broken.

Chapter 2400
Jia Lu didn't pay attention to Luo Zheng's actions. Instead, he focused all of his attention on the teleportation gate.
The moment Dongfang Chunjun led the Sages through the teleportation gate, Jia Lu's deep voice rang out. "Soldiers! It's time! "
Thousands of abyss devil envoys suddenly emitted dazzling golden light as they swarmed forward!
This golden light could prevent them from being hit by Dao Accumulations!
Although abyss devil envoys weren't strong enough to fight against Sages and were even weaker than Perfected True Gods, their unique talents made them extremely difficult to deal with.
Dongfang Chunjun had just stepped out of the teleportation gate when he saw this scene. His expression turned cold as he looked into the distance and saw Jia Lu.
"Perfected True Gods, I'll leave these abyss devil envoys to you!
"The rest of you, go kill the demons!
"Mu Haiji, Leng Yao, Tang Lun, follow me!"
The Grand Family Alliance was also divided into close and distant members. Mu Haiji, Leng Yao, and Tang Lun were the three people Dongfang Chunjun trusted the most!
After giving the order, the three Sages leaped up and transformed into three streaks of light as they flew toward Jia Lu.
As they flew across the Plains of Despair, dozens of giant birds gathered in the sky, trying to stop them. However, before these giant birds could even get close, they had already turned into balls of flames and fell from the sky, accompanied by shrill cries.
Tang Lun only glanced at them indifferently. These giant birds weren't qualified to stop them at all.
Jia Lu stood in place and didn't move. He only watched coldly as the three Sages rushed toward him!
At this moment, Mu Xuerong and the others had already transformed into streaks of bloody light and rushed toward the Plains of Despair.
The moment she stepped into the Plains of Despair, she felt the vitality in her body rapidly flowing away.
However, at her cultivation level, the vitality in her body was extremely abundant. This speed of loss was nothing …
Whoosh!
With a crisp sound, an abyss devil envoy's body was sliced in half.
These abyss devil envoys couldn't fight against Perfected True Gods, but they weren't afraid of Divine Abilities and could only kill them one by one.
Dongfang Taiqing strode forward. With a casual squeeze of his iron hand, an abyss devil envoy's head instantly exploded.
The other Perfected True Gods also attacked!
As for the Sages and other Sages, they directly bypassed these abyss devil envoys. Their targets were the demons.
Dongfang Chunjun stood in front of Jia Lu.
The difference in size between the two was like the difference between an elephant and a mosquito.
However, one side was the most powerful existence among humans, while the other side was the most powerful existence among demons.
However, Dongfang Chunjun's expression remained calm. "Let me take control of the Abyss Demonic Region. Your Demon Clan will still be the overlord of this place."
Jia Lu stared at the three Saints in front of him as if he was looking at three little bugs.
Jia Lu chuckled when he heard Dongfang Chunjun's words. "The Abyss Devil Realm originally belonged to my Abyss Devil Race. Do you think I will allow you to control this place?"
"I only want to borrow the power of the Abyssal Demonic Domain. I will not interfere with anything else," Dongfang Chunjun continued.
Jia Lu seemed to have heard an extremely ridiculous joke. "The power here will forever belong to the Evil Demon Clan. You humans … are nothing more than ants!"
Dongfang Chunjun looked at the Great Demon with a cold smile. "Then you can experience the power of an ant."
As soon as he said that, Dongfang Chunjun attacked!
"Buzz!"
A unique Taoist Connotation spread out from Dongfang Chunjun's hand. This Taoist Connotation formed many talismans. The talismans gathered together and quickly turned into a golden sword.
"This sword!"
Tang Lun and Mu Haiji's eyes narrowed.
Dongfang Chunjun rarely used weapons. The power contained in this sword was extremely terrifying. The Taoist Connotation used to communicate with this sword was so complicated that even the two Saints could not see through it.
"I have no interest in witnessing the power of an ant! Die! "
Jia Lu flipped his hand and a huge black ball appeared in his hand. He slammed it toward the three Saints.
"Hmph!"
Tang Lun snorted coldly. Raging flames surged up from his feet. His entire body had disappeared, and he had turned into a man of pure fire.
"Indestructible Flame Body!"
After turning into an Indestructible Flame Body, Tang Lun waved his hands. A wildly spinning ball of explosive flames shot out.
Mu Haiji also shook out a pure black longsword. An extremely powerful Taoist Connotation spread out in all directions. Then, it slashed fiercely toward Jia Lu.
A Saint's attack had the power to destroy the heavens and earth.
Moreover, these three Saints were the strongest in the Realm of the Gods …
"Boom!"
When Tang Lun and Mu Haiji's attacks collided with Jia Lu, the energy was highly condensed.
Even though the spatial laws in the Abyss Demonic Realm were completely ineffective, the surrounding space still began to collapse crazily.
…
…
On the Plains of Despair, the Aristocratic Alliance and the demons were locked in a fierce battle.
The demon commander facing Luo Zheng had already been beheaded.
Patriarch Qianlong looked at Luo Zheng in shock. He could not believe what he was seeing.
Powerful Taoist Connotation and power spread over from afar, but it did not affect Patriarch Qianlong at all.
Previously, Luo Zheng had crushed the black flames with one foot. The strength he displayed was extremely strong.
Even Patriarch Qianlong could not defeat this demon commander. Its strength was probably comparable to those peak quasi-sages. Yet, it was killed by Luo Zheng with one sword strike?
The Taoist Connotation in Luo Zheng's sword strike was extremely complex. It seemed to contain hundreds of Divine Daos. Patriarch Qianlong thought that his eyes were playing tricks on him.
"Let's go." Luo Zheng's gaze swept across the Plains of Despair.
"Whoosh!"
Taking advantage of this chaos, he even noticed Hua Tianming, who was fighting fiercely with the Abyss Demon Envoy.

After he released the strange fish, the few of them circled around the Plains of Despair and flew towards the huge Spatial Gate.
Looking at the Aristocratic Alliance's stance, it was difficult for them to take care of Luo Zheng's group.
Soon, they landed in front of the spatial door.
At this moment, Mu Xuerong, who had just killed several Abyss Demon Envoys, happened to look at Luo Zheng. Her beautiful eyes flickered with disbelief.
"Luo Zheng? Why is he here? "
Probably noticing Mu Xuerong's gaze, Luo Zheng turned around and smiled at Mu Xuerong when he stepped into the spatial door.
Mu Xuerong's heart turned cold. She wanted to keep Luo Zheng here, but on second thought, her current strength was far from being Luo Zheng's match. Moreover, the elders of her clan were fighting fiercely with the demons in the Plains of Despair …
This was a once-in-a-lifetime opportunity. How could she let him escape just like that?

Chapter 2401
There wasn't the slightest bit of spatial fluctuation in the large space door.
"How could this be …" Patriarch Qian Long said with a gloomy expression.
"Da da da da da …"
The strange fish shuttled back and forth in the space door. But without the support of the Laws, this space door was nothing more than a door frame. How could it shuttling back and forth like this have any effect?
"The Evil Demon Clan should have sealed the space door," Luo Zheng said with a frown.
This time, the Evil Demon Clan must have prepared for a long time for the invasion of the powerful alliance. Once the Saints entered the sixth floor, they cut off their escape route.
At the same time, they also cut off Luo Zheng's escape route.
"What should we do? Can we restore the space door? "Patriarch Qian Long asked.
Luo Zheng looked up at the space door. There were countless complex runes gathered around the door frame.
The Abyss Demon Domain forbade the Laws of Space. The Evil Demon Clan must have spent a lot of effort to build a space passage here. After they cut it off, it would be impossible to restore it in a short time!

Patriarch Qian Long and the other True Gods didn't mind. As long as the powerful alliance defeated the Evil Demon Clan, they shouldn't make things difficult for them.
But Luo Zheng was different!
No matter which side won, he wouldn't have a good end.
Just as Luo Zheng was thinking, Mu Xuerong's crisp voice suddenly spread out.
"Luo Zheng wants to escape through the Abyss Demon Domain!"
Although the battle between the Saint and the Evil Demon was deafening, and there was chaos everywhere.
But Mu Xuerong's voice, which was infused with true essence, was clearly transmitted to the ears of everyone on the Plains of Despair!
In Mu Xuerong's opinion, this battle wasn't difficult. As long as a Saint or a Quasi-Saint came out to stop Luo Zheng, it would be enough. This guy was too important. To some extent, he was even more important than the Abyss Demon Domain. So at this critical moment, she didn't care about anything else and spread the news.
"Luo Zheng? How could he be here? "Hua Tianming was stunned.
Even the powerful alliance had paid a great price to enter the Abyss Demon Domain. Several Quasi-Saints and Perfect True Gods had fallen.
How could Luo Zheng enter this forbidden area by himself?
Could it be that Luo Zheng had been following behind the Sages? This was even more impossible. How powerful was a Saint's divine sense? It was impossible for them to follow him for so long without noticing.
However, when Hua Tianming turned his head to look, his pupils suddenly shrank. He really saw Luo Zheng standing under the spatial door.
Emperor Hanqing turned his head and looked at Luo Zheng. His eyes were filled with doubt.
Just like Hua Tianming, he couldn't figure it out either …
However, this doubt only lasted for a moment before his eyes suddenly flashed with a bright light.
Among all the Saints present, he was the only one who knew the secret of the place of reincarnation.
Luo Zheng did not enter the Abyss Demon Domain with them, so he could only enter from another entrance, and that was the place of reincarnation!
After all, Emperor Hanqing had also left the place of reincarnation!
"I have arranged everything in the place of reincarnation. Even if I find the Stone Forest Rat, he will die without a doubt. How did Luo Zheng leave?" Emperor Hanqing's heart was filled with fear. "This kid … cannot be left alive!"
"Go to hell!"
A huge fiend commander swung a giant axe at Emperor Hanqing's head.
At this moment, the fiend commander's body was glowing with a violent red light.
This fiend commander's strength was close to that of a peak quasi-saint, but under the influence of the red light, he had the strength to fight a Saint.
Emperor Hanqing and the fiend commander had fought for dozens of rounds, and it was hard to tell who was the winner.
When the fiend commander saw that Emperor Hanqing was distracted, he exploded with all his strength, intending to take Emperor Hanqing's life!
"Hmph!"
Emperor Hanqing snorted and raised his hand.
"Ka!"
The two-hundred-meter-wide axe was blocked by Emperor Hanqing's hand!
The Fiend Commander was dumbfounded, its huge eyes filled with disbelief.
"Ka ka ka …"
A small crack appeared on the blade of the axe, and it quickly spread across the entire surface of the axe.
The axe was completely shattered, and Emperor Hanqing had only managed to block it slightly.
"Weng!"
Emperor Hanqing's eyes flashed, and he charged at the fiend commander with his sword in hand.
The fiend commander's mind spun quickly. He knew that the Saint in front of him was far stronger than he'd imagined. It turned out that his opponent hadn't been serious at all.
"Whoosh …"
That huge body also leaped up, wanting to escape.
But how could it be faster than Emperor Hanqing? Just as it was two steps away, it felt a chill on its stomach.
At the same time, a calm voice transmitted over.
"One Sword … Severing Heaven and Earth!"
This sword strike actually faintly contained the True Meaning of the Dao.
Just like that, the three to four-hundred-meter-tall body was cleanly sliced in half. It couldn't fight back at all!
Emperor Hanqing didn't spend so much effort to join the Wealthy Alliance to help Dongfang Chunjun control the Abyss Devil Region. Besides, if he showed off too much strength, Dongfang Chunjun would definitely be wary of him.
When he fought the fiend commander, he didn't put in any effort.
But now, things were different. He had to capture Luo Zheng first!
Just as Emperor Hanqing was about to head straight for Luo Zheng, a huge explosion sounded out from behind him.
"Bang!"
Following a flash of light, a figure shot out, smashing a huge crater in the ground in front of Emperor Hanqing.
Dongfang Chunjun stood in the crater. He'd taken Jia Lu's attack head-on. His clothes were slightly tattered, but his eyes were filled with battle intent.
He glanced at Emperor Hanqing, then said, "Brother Hanqing, you're very powerful. These fiend commanders aren't easy to deal with. I'll have to ask you to take care of them!"
Then, Dongfang Chunjun transformed into a gust of wind, charging toward Jia Lu at the end of the Plains of Despair.
"That old fox!" Emperor Hanqing was furious.
He knew that Dongfang Chunjun was wary of him, but right now, Dongfang Chunjun, Tang Lun, and Mu Haiji were busy dealing with Jia Lu. This was his best chance!
But Dongfang Chunjun was clearly trying to keep him on the battlefield.
Seeing that Luo Zheng had already fled to the left of the Plains of Despair, he had an idea.
"Catch him …"
Han Liusu, Han Chuyue, and Han Cangyan were also fighting against the Abyss Devil Envoys. The three of them worked together as if they were one person.
Even though the three of them had just entered the Complete True God Realm, when they worked together, they were much stronger than ordinary Complete True Gods!

Chapter 2402
The Plains of Despair was very chaotic.
Although the Sages were caught in a fierce battle, they had a considerable advantage in the overall situation.
However, the Sages also knew that the Abyss Demon Area was not so easy to take down.
Since the Evil Demons were willing to fight here, they must have some tricks up their sleeves.
Dongfang Chunjun was involved with Jia Lu, but he was also quietly observing the situation.

After Dongfang Chunjun noticed Luo Zheng, he was also confused. Wasn't this kid too amazing? How could he come to such a damned place?

This Luo Zheng was merely at the Perfection Stage. What right did he have to appear here?
But it was also good …
One of the main reasons he was anxious to take control of the Abyss Demon Area was to fight against those Nirvana Realm experts from outside the area!
If he could capture Luo Zheng, it would be a heavy bargaining chip. At the very least, he could understand those Nirvana Realm experts through Luo Zheng.
In the blink of an eye, these messy thoughts flashed through his mind.
Then his lips moved slightly, and he had already sent out a True Yuan transmission …
A Secondary Sage from the Dongfang Family, the Tang Family, and the Mu Family each received Dongfang Chunjun's transmission.

Clan Leader Qianlong knew some of the Secondary Sages on the Plains of Despair.

Clan Leader Qianlong looked in the direction Luo Zheng had fled. There were already seven or eight Perfection Stage experts chasing after him.
It looked like what Luo Zheng had said was true. He did indeed have a connection with the Wealthy Alliance, and it was not a small one. Otherwise, the Wealthy Alliance would not have sent people to chase after him at such a crucial time.
It was just that Luo Zheng's strength far exceeded that of an ordinary Perfect True God.

Just as Patriarch Qian Long was pondering, three more figures emerged from the Plains of Despair.
"Three quasi-saints …"

A pinnacle Perfected True God, such as Dongfang Taiqing, would not be outdone by an ordinary Second Sage.
It was impossible for a non-wealthy Second Sage like Patriarch Qianlong to defeat Dongfang Taiqing.
Meanwhile, Dongfang Qiu, Mu Tianshou, and Tang Chou, these three quasi-Sages, were top-notch existences even among the many Secondary Sages in wealthy families!
They were also quasi-Sages like Patriarch Qianlong, but their strength was far superior!
"These three people have actually come out. Brother Luo Zheng … I'm afraid it's not good for him!"
Patriarch Qianlong could not help but sigh at this thought.
Even if he wanted to, he was powerless now. He could only silently hope that Luo Zheng could escape safely!
...
...

There was one thing he was very sure of. Even if he fell into the hands of the Fiend Devils, it would be better than falling into Dongfang Chunjun's hands!
After crossing the mountain range on the left side of the Plains of Despair, there was a huge sea of lava in front of him.
Without any other choice, Luo Zheng did not hesitate at all. He flew on the surface of the sea of lava and spread out his divine sense at the same time.
"Someone is chasing after me!"
The moment Luo Zheng spread out his divine sense, he had already sensed three auras behind him.
Those were the auras of Perfected True Gods!

When he had just returned to the Divine Territory, Luo Zheng had already displayed his strength.
Dongfang Chunjun should know that Perfected True Gods were not qualified to keep him here. Not to mention three people, even 30 people might not have the ability to do so.
Luo Zheng could not be bothered with them. He just flew on this vast sea of lava...
But what Luo Zheng did not know was that on the right side of the Plains of Despair was the Fiend Race's ranch. The ferocious creatures there were basically their food.
And on the left side was the famous Sea of Despair.
Even the Fiends were not willing to set foot in this damned place!

"Thunder Form Manifestation!"

But just as Luo Zheng was flying, a few huge creatures suddenly popped out from the sea of lava and blocked Luo Zheng's way!
"Hualala …"
Rolling lava splashed everywhere.
They were slender and huge snake heads. The surface of each snake head had a metallic luster. They faced Luo Zheng and exuded a ferocious aura.
"Nine-headed Snake that lives in the sea of lava?"
Luo Zheng was not interested in deducing which legend the Nine-headed Snake came from.
Before the snake heads could bite him, Luo Zheng had already swung his sword a few times.
"Swish swish swish swish …"
While the sword lights were flying in the air, Luo Zheng had already spread his thunder wings. He quickly increased his altitude and continued to fly forward.
He did not have time to play with these ferocious creatures.
"Roar! Roar … "

Their speed was even faster than Luo Zheng's. Three of the heads even circled in front of Luo Zheng to block Luo Zheng's way!
"Troublesome!"
"Eight Turns Flying Smoke!"
"True Meaning of Dao Technique!"
At this moment, Luo Zheng's body became extremely agile. He turned a strange path in the air and swept past the snake heads.
The nine snake heads opened their bloody mouths and bit repeatedly, but they could only bite the afterimage behind Luo Zheng.
But when the Nine-headed Snake blocked him …
The pursuers behind him were getting closer to Luo Zheng.

Chapter 2403
Han Jia's trio formed a special formation. Han Liusu was the leader, while Han Cangyan and Han Chuyue were flanking her.
The three of them came at an incredible speed. Luo Zheng paused for a moment under the Hydra's obstruction, but the three of them had already caught up to him!
In the land of reincarnation, Luo Zheng already knew the secret of Emperor Han Qing. He also knew that the memories of the three of them had been erased. The three of them were not the three he knew back then.
Luo Zheng sized up the three of them, but he didn't stop.
Now, only the three from Han Jia had followed him. If he waited any longer, they wouldn't be Perfect True Gods, but quasi-sages, or Sages.
He wanted to help Han Liusu and the others recover their memories, but now wasn't the time.
Luo Zheng passed through the nine heads like a cloud, then fled. This action seemed to have angered the nine-headed snake.
Since they couldn't catch up to Luo Zheng, they vented their anger on Han Jia's trio.
"Awoo! Awoo … "
All the snake heads opened at the same time, spitting out balls of blood at Han Liusu and the others!
The eyes of the leader, Han Liusu, flashed with purple light. The formation the three had set up suddenly flashed with a faint purple light.
Han Chuyue and Han Cangyan's Dao was transmitted to Han Liusu through the formation!
"Violet Flame Sword!"
This formation was actually a joint attack formation, which was personally set up by Emperor Han Qing.
Han Jia's three sons were of one mind. Together with this formation, their strength increased by several times!
Seeing the nine-headed snake spit out balls of blood, Han Liusu waved the violet flame sword in her hand.
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
The violet flame sword instantly split into dozens of smaller swords.
These small swords shot out in all directions. Some of the small swords pierced through the blood-colored ball of light and burned it clean, while even more of the small swords shot towards the nine-headed snake.
The nine-headed snake was huge, but its speed was extremely slow.
In the blink of an eye, the swords pierced into the snake's body.
These swords were only the size of a palm, while each of the nine-headed snake's heads was hundreds of meters long and dozens of meters wide. Thus, they didn't seem to be injured at all.
The purple light in Han Liusu's eyes flashed once more. She softly spat out a word, "Explode!"
"Hua!"
Blazing purple flames instantly spread out from the necks of the nine heads.
The huge Hydra howled as it fell into the sea of lava.
The purple flames were also incomparably ferocious. Even after mixing into the magma, they continued to burn fiercely.
In the blink of an eye, the nine-headed snake was burned to a crisp. Even the surface of the magma sea formed a purple flame that soared into the sky.
"After Liusu stepped into the Great Perfection stage, the purple Heavenly Fire became even more powerful!"

Chapter 2408

Chapter 2411
