But now it was time to verify it …
"My name is Luo Zheng," Luo Zheng said with a Quintessential Sound Transmission.
When Luo Zheng said this, the hand holding Luo Zheng suddenly shook, then asked, "You're from the Great Evolution Realm?"
Luo Zheng said with a smile, "You're Chen Huang Yijian? The master of Slaughter Sword Mountain! "
Strictly speaking, Chen Huang Yijian could be considered Luo Zheng's half master. After all, the Yi God Sword was passed down from him.
The other party was silent for a while. Then, he chuckled. That laughter was full of vicissitudes of life, as if the depression that had been brewing for countless years had finally disappeared completely.

Chapter 2413
Before, Luo Zheng was surrounded by thick black water, so he naturally couldn't see Chen Huang Yijian.
But after coming to this space, Luo Zheng really saw Chen Huang Yijian's true body … …
This Yi Jian Tianzun was unkempt and unkempt, and his clothes were tattered, like a beggar.
He couldn't be mentioned in the same breath as the Chen Huang Yijian that Luo Zheng saw in the Emperor's Corridor.
The only similarity between the two was that pair of sharp black eyes. This pair of black eyes were like gems, flashing with a dark light.
"It shouldn't be the first time you've seen me," Chen Huang Yijian said as he turned back.
Luo Zheng said, "I once saw you in the Emperor's Corridor in the Illusion Battlefield."
Chen Huang Yijian nodded slightly, that was a soul imprint he had left behind.
But the soul imprint he left behind at that time was not as good as Yu Shenfeng's, so Yu Shenfeng's soul in the Immortal Mansion was stimulated and knew of Luo Zheng's existence, but Chen Huang Yijian didn't know before.
Chen Huang Yijian stared at Luo Zheng and continued to ask, "Who are you to the Firmament Sacred Sect?"
Although Chen Huang Yijian already had an eighty to ninety percent answer to Luo Zheng's identity, he still had to confirm it.
"Luo Xiao is my father," Luo Zheng replied.
"Sure enough … …" Chen Huang Yijian said matter-of-factly, "Then in the Emperor's Corridor, you didn't take me as your master?"
"Yes," Luo Zheng nodded and said, "Before that, I had already taken North Sage as my master."
"That should be the case. Among the ten Dao Children after me, you are the most important one. North Sage's Immortal Mansion was left for you … …" Chen Huang Yijian murmured.
When Chen Huang Yijian was still a Celestial Venerable, he didn't know about the Immortal Mansion's existence.
When he knew about the Dao Children's plan, Chen Huang Yijian had already reached the Divine Realm.
Luo Zheng sized up the surrounding space. This space was very narrow and round. The outermost layer of the space was still slowly fluctuating. He asked, "Have you been living in the Sea of Meditation all these years?"
Chen Huang Yijian bitterly smiled, "It should be said that we live in this narrow bubble. In the Black Water Domain, there are only four bubbles like this … If we live outside the bubble, we will be swallowed by the unknown sooner or later!"
"Those 'unknown things' can't enter the bubble?" Luo Zheng asked.
Chen Huang Yijian nods, "Not only those 'unknown things', even other demons cannot enter this bubble. This bubble only belongs to me."
Back then, when Chen Huang Yijian was running all the way in the Abyss Devil Domain, it was also by chance that he entered the Black Water Region.
The monster that was chasing after Chen Huang Yijian was too powerful. With Chen Huang Yijian's strength, he was unable to fight back. He could only dive into the black waters.
Unexpectedly, those ferocious creatures were extremely afraid of the black water. Not a single one of them chased after them …
But Chen Huang Yijian quickly encountered the 'unknown fear' in the black water.
The fear released by those 'unknown things' could not be resisted at all!
Everyone would make a different choice under extreme fear …
Some people would tremble like an ostrich, staying in place and not daring to escape.
Just like Luo Zheng, under extreme fear, he would prepare a full-strength attack in an attempt to eliminate the source of this fear.
But Chen Huang Yijian chose to escape.
In this sticky black water, it was also due to luck that he safely escaped from the mouths of those 'unknown things'. In the end, he actually drilled into a bubble.
This bubble was the space where Luo Zheng was.
"Later, I learned that these bubbles were created by the demons after spending a lot of effort. They want to cultivate in the Black Water Domain. Without the protection of these bubbles, they will die without a doubt," Chen Huang Yijian said as he reached out and gently tapped the edge of the bubble.
A wisp of black water was dragged into the bubble. With a gentle twist of his finger, this wisp of black water was repelled outside the bubble. He shook his head and smiled bitterly. "Until now, I still don't know how the demons made these bubbles. This race is too talented …"
"Are there no other demons who came to trouble you?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
After all, the Black Water Region belonged to the Abyss Demon Realm. How could the demons just watch Chen Huang Yijian occupy their things?
"Of course there are," Chen Huang Yijian shrugged. "Unfortunately, these bubbles have been refined by me. Other than cursing outside, what can they do to me?"
These bubbles should be regarded as some kind of special space magic treasure.
After the most powerful demons obtained this water-repelling magic treasure, they used the Black Water Region as their own cultivation place.
At that time, they thought that it was difficult for others to enter the Abyss Demon Realm, let alone enter the black water, so they did not refine these bubbles …
Very accidentally, Chen Huang Yijian took advantage of this loophole.
"There was a period of time when I was annoyed by the cursing. I had to plant a divine pattern to resist the outside Divine Sense," Chen Huang Yijian smiled helplessly. "So if this bubble hadn't given me a place to live, I'm afraid I would have been reincarnated …"
"Reincarnated?" Hearing Chen Huang Yijian's words, Luo Zheng's eyes suddenly flashed. "You can reincarnate?"
Chen Huang Yijian nodded slightly. With a gentle flick of his hand, a green light bloomed in his hand.
It was a sparkling green butterfly!
Although Luo Zheng had never seen this butterfly, he still called out the butterfly's name. "This is a green scale butterfly! I didn't expect it to be in your hands! "
"You have been to the place of reincarnation," Chen Huang Yijian said.
"Well, I was transferred from the place of reincarnation to the Abyss Demon Realm," Luo Zheng said.
There were three altars in the place of reincarnation …
Previously, Luo Zheng was puzzled. There were no statues on the other two altars, so he didn't know where the green scale butterfly and the flaming blood eagle were. He didn't expect that one of them was in Chen Huang Yijian's hands!
Chen Huang Yijian, who was hiding here, had no other cultivation resources. His cultivation method was similar to Han Liusu's. He used the green scale butterfly to get close to the flame of memory, and absorbed the cultivation memories in the flame of memory little by little. Only then did he have his current quasi-sage cultivation step by step.
"Why were you chased by Emperor Han Qing and the others when you were transferred from the place of reincarnation to the Abyss Demon Realm?" Chen Huang Yijian was somewhat puzzled.
Although Chen Huang Yijian had the green scale butterfly, he couldn't use it at will like Luo Zheng. He couldn't search the flame of memory, and couldn't always know the movements of the Divine Realm.
Luo Zheng told Chen Huang Yijian some of the information he knew.

Chapter 2414
"Opportunity? What opportunity? "Luo Zheng's face showed a trace of confusion.
Chen Huang Yijian's eyes glowed.
Sword cultivators were very stubborn. Once they decided on something, they would definitely do it.
Chen Huang Yijian used to be righteous, but after being corroded by the black water for so many years, not only was his body damaged, even his temperament became more stubborn.
"A chance to control the Abyss Demon Area."
Chen Huang Yijian raised his eyebrows and looked at Luo Zheng up and down. "In this perfect opportunity, a perfect candidate has appeared. This is simply fate's arrangement!"
Chen Huang Yijian was already a quasi saint and couldn't control the forbidden area. Even if such an opportunity appeared, he couldn't grasp it.
And Luo Zheng was a perfect paragon. Naturally, he was very suitable!
"Control the Abyss Demon Area …"
Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed slightly.
This time, not only were there many saints and quasi saints, there were also many perfect true gods. Hua Tianming, Mu Xuerong, and the others had all come …
Their goal was to use one of them to control the Abyss Demon Area.
Just now, Luo Zheng had fought with the three sons of the Han Family. With the help of 9527, he had removed the bindings of Han Liusu and Han Chuyue. But Han Cangyan had quickly escaped. Emperor Han Qing must have wanted to leave one person behind to control this place.
The Abyss Demon Area was the head of the forbidden areas. If he could control it, it would be a great help.
"But to take control of the Abyss Demon Area from the hands of Dongfang Chunjun and Emperor Han Qing, I'm afraid it won't be that easy." Luo Zheng shook his head.
The other side had dozens of saints and quasi saints. There was no way to fight them head-on.
Other things aside …
If it wasn't for the black water and Chen Huang Yijian's protection, Luo Zheng would have been dead. Unless he used the Hermit ability to escape, he might not have been able to escape.
"Emperor Han Qing is very strong. Dongfang Chunjun is also very strong. But we don't have to fight them head-on," Chen Huang Yijian said. "Even if we have no choice, we might still be able to fight them head-on!"
"Being trapped in the Meditation Sea, your strength has indeed improved greatly. There aren't many people in the Realm of the Gods who can directly block Emperor Hanqing," Luo Zheng said with a nod.
Emperor Hanqing's strength was already at the peak of the Saints' level.
Although he had not seen his true strength, he should not be too far off from Dongfang Chunjun.
Even among the saints, Emperor Hanqing was a top figure. But Chen Huang Yijian was only a quasi-saint, yet he had such strength. Even Luo Zheng was quite surprised.
One had to know that Chen Huang Yijian was only a "secondary creature" that had walked out of the universe, and Luo Xiao was only a Saint.
Chen Huang Yijian shook his head slightly and said, "This is also within the Abyssal Demon Region. If it was outside the Abyssal Demon Region, I would not be able to match Emperor Hanqing."
"What do you mean?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
"You have been to the Slaughtering Sword Mountain before. Do you know my cultivation method back then?" Chen Huangyi asked.
Luo Zheng nodded, "Using the Slaughtering Sword Mountain to absorb evil energy, using evil energy to suppress emotions, using it to cut emotions …"
The real Cutting Emotions Divine Path definitely could not be cultivated using this method. That was just Chen Huang Yijian's helplessness in the Dayan Universe, but it was still an extremely exquisite move.
Luo Zheng had also benefited greatly from the Slaughtering Sword Mountain. It was also because of this trip that he formed a connection with Xun.
"I relied on the Jade Scale Butterfly to increase my cultivation, forming the One Thought Sword Realm and becoming a quasi-saint … but this strength was still not enough in the Abyssal Demon Region." Chen Huang Yijian said, "Later I thought of another method, a method similar to the Slaughtering Sword Mountain!"
For a Perfect True God to become a quasi-saint, they must cultivate their own heaven and earth. This heaven and earth were fundamentally different from the inner world.
For example, Aunt Hanjiu used the Purple Cloud Divine Path to create the Purple Sky Absolute Land, while Chen Huang Yijian created the One Thought Sword Realm.
Whether it was the Purple Sky Absolute Land or the One Thought Sword Realm, a part of it could be projected into the Realm of the Gods.
"The method of the Slaughtering Sword Mountain? Could it be that you created another Slaughtering Sword Mountain here? "Luo Zheng was somewhat puzzled.
The baleful aura in the Realm of the Gods was not considered powerful. Other than the Killing God Path which could be slightly used, the other Divine Paths could not be used.
"How is that possible?" Chen Huang Yijian laughed. "You can think of the Meditation Sea as a Slaughtering Sword Mountain! After becoming a quasi-saint, I refined 128 swords and inserted them into the Meditation Sea, allowing them to absorb the power of the entire Meditation Sea for my own use! "
The Slaughtering Sword Mountain used swords to transmit the baleful aura within.
The Meditation Sea originally contained a primordial Terror power. Chen Huangyi had used his original idea to use the primordial Terror power of the Meditation Sea as baneful aura.
"So it's like this …" Luo Zheng's eyes shone.
He was indeed puzzled before. Chen Huang Yijian was only a quasi-saint, how could he block Emperor Hanqing's spear …
The Emotion Severing Divine Path might be one reason, but the deeper reason was probably the "Meditation Sea".
"Young Master also entered the Dao through the Emotion Severing Divine Path. Although you have not cultivated the One Thought Sword Domain, you can still use the power of the Meditation Sea. I can help you set up a sword formation," Chen Huang Yijian suddenly said.
Chen Huang Yijian called Luo Zheng "Young Master", which made Luo Zheng feel slightly strange.
Luo Zheng did not know what the relationship between Chen Huang Yijian and his father was. Was "Chen Huang Yijian" also one of the Daoists, or was he a Daoist of the previous generation?
But these were all trivial matters, so Luo Zheng naturally did not ask.
"I can also set up a sword formation?" Luo Zheng's eyes lit up slightly.
According to Chen Huang Yijian, this primordial Fear Force could only be used in the Abyss Demon Realm, but it was enough.
Now that the path to the sixth floor was closed, Luo Zheng could not leave.
If it was really like what Chen Huang Yijian said, to find a way to take control of the Abyss Demon Realm from the Wealthy Alliance, strengthening himself would become very necessary …
"If Young Master is willing, of course you can." Chen Huang Yijian nodded.
With that, he took a step forward and came to the center of the bubble …
He stretched out his hand and gently patted, and a long table lay horizontally in the bubble.
Then Chen Huang Yijian gently swiped, accompanied by a burst of "ding ding ding ding" sounds. Long swords of different styles and different lengths appeared on the long table.
"It's just that the quality of these long swords is poor. I'm afraid they can't withstand the corrosion of the Meditation Sea." Chen Huang Yijian's face showed a trace of helplessness.
Although there were countless longswords in his One Thought Sword Domain, they could only be projected when he used the One Thought Sword Domain. It was impossible for him to directly take out the divine domain. If he took out the longswords from the sword domain, they would also be reduced to Quintessential Essence.

Chapter 2415
As a swordsman, Chen Huang Yijian was obsessed with all kinds of treasured swords.
But he had not been in the Realm of the Gods for long, and he only had three long swords that could be called grandmist treasures.
However, grandmist treasures were also divided into high and low. His three long swords were also considered ordinary.
Apart from these three long swords, the other long swords were also ordinary objects in the Realm of the Gods. It was not difficult for ordinary Gods to obtain one.
"I only have these. I wonder if Young Master has a spare sword?" Chen Huang Yijian looked back at Luo Zheng and asked.
"Naturally," Luo Zheng smiled slightly.
Then he imitated Chen Huang Yijian and walked to the long table. At the same time, he reached out and gently touched the Sumeru Ring in his hand.
Long swords were taken out of the Sumeru Ring and scattered on the long table.
From the Realm of the Gods to the Desolate Bone Land, Luo Zheng did not deliberately collect those treasures.
But if there were spoils of war, he would still quietly put them away, and wait until he had time to slowly organize them.
Some ordinary objects that did not catch his eye, he directly threw them away.
Just like some ordinary grandmist treasures, he even left them in the Immortal Mansion …
Even so, the number of magic treasures he had accumulated over the years was still not small.
"Clang clang clang clang clang …"
Long swords slipped out of the Sumeru Ring on Luo Zheng's fingertips.
When Chen Huang Yijian saw those long swords, his eyes suddenly went wide!
"Grandmist treasures!"
"There are dozens of long swords, all of them are grandmist treasures …"
"This brown long sword seems to be a chaos treasure! What is the name of this sword? "
Looking at Chen Huang Yijian's excited expression, Luo Zheng showed a helpless expression and only replied, "I don't remember …"
This brown long sword should have been taken out from the Sumeru Ring of some Desolate God. He naturally did not know its name.
In any case, this fellow was a quasi-saint …
According to Chen Huang Yijian's current strength, these things were simply scrap metal to him. Why was he so excited?!
Luo Zheng's idea was not wrong.
With Chen Huang Yijian's current cultivation and strength, these swords were not worth his attention.
However, when Chen Huang Yijian stepped into the Divine Realm, it was when the Luo Family was on the verge of collapse. He didn't stay for too long before he was forced to flee into the Abyssal Demon Domain …
Only if he had the chance to collect all kinds of treasured swords in God's Domain!
However, Chen Huang Yijian himself was a sword maniac. Seeing Luo Zheng suddenly throw out these swords, his eyes naturally lit up.
Chen Huangyi said 128 long swords. Luo Zheng took out more than 100 long swords from his spatial ring and stacked them on the table.
"Is this enough?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Of course it's enough!" Chen Huang Yijian stretched out his hand and stroked one of the swords. It was a sword that Luo Zheng had obtained from the Sword Race. The Sword Race loved the way of the sword, so their sword-making techniques were naturally extraordinary. It immediately attracted Chen Huang Yijian's attention. "Can you gift this sword to me?"
"If you like it, you can take it." Luo Zheng nodded with a smile.
Chen Huang Yijian's face under his disheveled hair suddenly revealed a child-like smile. True love for swords, to this point …
"Do I have to refine these swords one by one?" Luo Zheng asked again.
Chen Huang Yijian nodded. "I'll help you refine them. Later, when these swords are pierced into the Meditation Sea, you can activate them!"
As he spoke, he stretched out his hand and gently patted the table.
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
All the swords jumped up from the table.
One of Chen Huangyi's hands moved rapidly, leaving a thumb-sized imprint on the hilt of each sword.
In less than ten breaths of time, Chen Huang Yijian was ready.
He said to Luo Zheng, "Take these swords and follow me into the Black Water."
"Still want to enter the Black Water?" Luo Zheng frowned.
Although under Chen Huang Yijian's description, he had a general understanding of the Meditation Sea.
But the "unknown things" hidden in the Black Water, as well as the primitive fear power, still made Luo Zheng uncomfortable.
Sensing Luo Zheng's mood, Chen Huang Yijian smiled. "Don't worry, now we have already sneaked into the depths of the Meditation Sea."
"The depths of the Meditation Sea? There are no 'unknown things' here? "Luo Zheng asked curiously.
"No … it can be said that they are everywhere," Chen Huang Yijian replied.
"Then what should we do?" Luo Zheng asked again.
"Naturally, we have to rely on this thing," Chen Huang Yijian said, suddenly taking out a triangular gold nugget.
Chen Huang Yijian gently brushed the surface of the gold nugget. The surface of the gold nugget seemed to burn, blooming with dazzling golden light that reflected in the entire bubble. Luo Zheng and Chen Huang Yijian's faces seemed to be plated with a layer of gold powder, very dazzling.
At the same time, Luo Zheng felt a trace of warmth from this gold nugget.
"This warm current … I felt it before," Luo Zheng said as he looked at the gold nugget.
Chen Huang Yijian nodded. "Previously, your state of mind was suppressed by fear, so I used the 'Grass Flame Gold' to help you get rid of it. When walking in the upper level of the Meditation Sea, you don't need this thing, but I will take you to set up the sword array, so naturally, this thing is indispensable."
This Grass Flame Gold was also a kind of strange object born in the Meditation Sea …
At the bottom of the Meditation Sea, there were some extremely powerful "unknown things" everywhere. When encountering these "unknown things", even the most powerful demons would die without a doubt.
But these "unknown things" were extremely afraid of the light emitted by the Grass Flame Gold.
As long as the light of the Grass Flame Gold was not extinguished, walking on the bottom of the sea would not be hindered.
"Follow me closely!"
Chen Huang Yijian said as he walked out of the bubble with the Grass Flame Gold in his hand.
Seeing this, Luo Zheng stretched out his hand and gently brushed the long table. The long swords refined by Chen Huang Yijian were put into his Sumeru Ring one after another.
"Gudong …"
Luo Zheng followed closely behind Chen Huang Yijian and once again drilled into the viscous black water.
Previously, when Luo Zheng descended, he could not see his five fingers in front of him.
But this time, after Chen Huang Yijian activated the Grass Flame Gold, the golden light of the Grass Flame Gold spread out in the black water and illuminated an area of about ten feet!
"Don't leave the light of the Grass Flame Gold. These unknown things are very sensitive. They will slowly gather and follow behind us. Once you leave the range of this golden light, they will attack together!" Chen Huang Yijian said.
"Didn't you say that these 'unknown things' have no form? Then how do you know they attacked? "Luo Zheng thought of this and asked.
Chen Huang Yijian thought for a while before answering, "Once, I was chased by demons in the Meditation Sea … The demon's Grass Flame Gold was burned out. When the golden light was extinguished, it naturally attracted those 'unknown things'."
Speaking of this matter, Chen Huang Yijian seemed to have a lingering fear. Even demons were extremely afraid of these unknown things.

Chapter 2416
The light emitted by the Grass Flame Gold was extremely strong, but it could not spread far in the black water.
However, the primitive fear power in the undercurrent had been dispelled by the golden light emitted by the Grass Flame Gold. Luo Zheng naturally did not feel the slightest discomfort.
"Gudong, gudong …"
As they continued to dive in the black water, the undercurrents became more and more intense.
Some of the undercurrents actually went straight to Luo Zheng, wanting to sweep Luo Zheng away from Chen Huang Yijian.
"Hurry up, these guys are eyeing us covetously. If you are swept away, you will lose your life," Chen Huang Yijian said.
Luo Zheng nodded and followed closely behind Chen Huang Yijian.
The two of them seemed to be moving forward in a dark forest, only relying on the light of the gold to carefully move forward. If the light of the gold was extinguished, they would immediately be swallowed by the greedy beasts.
Chen Huang Yijian's bubble was suspended in the middle of the Meditation Sea.
The depth of the Meditation Sea was obviously beyond Luo Zheng's expectations …
Their speed wasn't slow, but it still took them an hour to reach the bottom.
Not long after, Luo Zheng finally felt his feet touch the bottom of the sea.
When Luo Zheng released his Divine Sense, he found that there was a huge trench in front of him. He did not know how far this trench extended down.
Fortunately, Chen Huang Yijian said, "We're here, the first sword can be inserted there."
Chen Huang Yijian pointed to the side of the trench …
Luo Zheng's Divine Sense followed the direction Chen Huang Yijian pointed to and soon found a sharp sword inserted into the surface of the trench.
"Okay!"
Luo Zheng obediently took out a long sword.
According to Chen Huang Yijian's demonstration, he activated the mark at the bottom of the long sword and then threw the long sword towards the surface of the trench!
"Gulu gulu …"
Although the black water was very viscous and this place was in the deep waters, the water pressure was very high.
But it could not withstand Luo Zheng's strength. The long sword flew towards the trench with a string of bubbles!
"Dong!"
After a while, a muffled sound was heard.
Luo Zheng and Chen Huang Yijian's Divine Senses simultaneously swept towards the long sword. The long sword was already firmly inserted into the body of the sword. Soon after, Chen Huang Yijian gave Luo Zheng a thumbs up, indicating that Luo Zheng had done a good job.
The sword had been refined by Chen Huangyi. After being activated by Luo Zheng and inserted into the trench, it formed a subtle connection with Luo Zheng's mind.
Luo Zheng smiled, and then scattered his Divine Sense upward …
Previously, he had not released his divine sense because he was afraid of those unknown things. Now that Chen Huangyi's sword did not stop him, it meant that there was no danger in releasing his divine sense.
"Eh?"
Under Luo Zheng's probing, the area above him was empty. Other than the black water, there was nothing else!
This was completely different from what Luo Zheng felt.
It must be known that he and Chen Huangyi's sword had attracted many unknown things to follow them.
According to Luo Zheng's inference, these unknown things were of different sizes, flying back and forth above his head. Some of the larger unknown things were still tirelessly creating intense undercurrents, trying to roll Luo Zheng out of the range of the golden light of the "Grass Flame Gold".
But in his Divine Sense, they did not exist at all?
"As I said, there is no way to detect the existence of these 'unknown things' because they themselves are the 'unknown fear'. So you can only feel their existence, but you can't prove their existence …" Chen Huangyi noticed Luo Zheng's suspicion and explained to Luo Zheng.
"Can't prove their existence …"
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. He did not know the meaning of the existence of the Sea of Meditation. Did it have a special function like the Land of Reincarnation, or was it just a dangerous place?
After the first long sword was inserted, the triangular "Grass Flame Gold" was almost "burned", from the size of a fist to the size of a thumb.
The range of the golden light also shrunk from a few feet to a few feet.
Luo Zheng also clearly felt that the "unknown things" hidden in the black water were getting closer …
Chen Huangyi took out another "Grass Flame Gold" from the Sumeru Ring and gently rubbed it. The light of the second "Grass Flame Gold" suddenly brightened.
At the same time, he threw another "Grass Flame Gold" to Luo Zheng. "Take this" Grass Flame Gold "in case you need it …"
Luo Zheng nodded. After putting away the "Grass Flame Gold", he walked along the trench to the left.
But just as he took two steps, Luo Zheng felt that the black water in front of him suddenly transmitted a strong spiral!
"Hualala …"
A stream of water spiraled straight toward Luo Zheng.
This spiral of water was extraordinarily powerful, and its speed was also incomparably fast!
When Luo Zheng reacted, the spiral of water had already wrapped around Luo Zheng's body. His whole body was constantly spiraling with the current, and at the same time, he was pushed back by the current!
"Oh no!"
Seeing this scene, Chen Huangyi's complexion suddenly changed.
Once Luo Zheng was pushed out of the range of the "Grass Flame Gold" by the spiral of water, he would undoubtedly die!
At the same time that Luo Zheng was being swept away, Chen Huangyi's body twisted and was also swept up by the spiral of water.
In this way, the two of them flew together in the water, and the speed of their retreat was also the same. And Luo Zheng was also shrouded in the golden light of the "Grass Flame Gold" …
"Hualala …"
After the spiral of water pushed Luo Zheng and Chen Huangyi more than ten li away, the power of the spiral began to weaken, and Luo Zheng and Chen Huangyi broke free from the water.
"The power of this water is so strong, what is it?" Luo Zheng curiously asked.
The power in the water was very weak! Especially in this viscous black water, it was difficult to exert any kind of power.
Even if one had the strength of ten thousand catties, in this black water, they would only be able to exert one thousand catties of power …
Even if Luo Zheng went all out, he would not be able to create such a powerful spiral of water.
"It is also an unknown thing," Chen Huangyi looked at the darkness with a serious face, "But this unknown thing is very powerful. The Demons call this unknown thing 'Evil'! We are in trouble … "
Evil …
Luo Zheng chewed on this word, and then said, "Since this thing is also an unknown thing, it should also be afraid of the Grass Flame Gold, right?"
"Of course," Chen Huangyi said in a deep voice, "But this thing is too powerful. Once it has its eyes on you, even with the protection of the Grass Flame Gold, it will be difficult to escape from its hands!"
As soon as Chen Huangyi's voice fell, he stretched out his hand and suddenly pulled Luo Zheng's shoulder.
"Xiu!"
This time, an extremely fine spiral of water flew over, passing over Luo Zheng's head.
If it were not for Chen Huangyi's pull, this needle of water would have penetrated Luo Zheng's head …

Chapter 2417
Luo Zheng's brows furrowed slightly, and he felt that the water in the distance was constantly gathering into tiny spirals.
These tiny spirals were like slender needles. After gathering, they shot toward the two of them!
"Let's go!"
The two of them reminded each other at the same time, then turned their heads and continued to move forward in the black water.
"Water Controlling Cloud!"
The Taoist Connotation in Luo Zheng's body bloomed.
This was the Water Controlling Technique of the Li Water Divine Dao. It formed a smooth water film on the surface of his body.
Although it was greatly reduced in the black water, it could still speed up Luo Zheng's escape.
Chen Huangyi's escape speed was not slow at this moment, but those tiny spirals were faster!
"Whoosh whoosh …"
There were hundreds of tiny spirals behind them. These spirals seemed to weave into a fine fishing net, enveloping the two of them.
"Wah wah …"
At the same time, Luo Zheng heard a baby-like cry coming from the black water.
"This cry …"
"It's the sound of evil laughter, like the cry of a three-year-old child." Chen Huangyi's expression was solemn.
The Meditation Sea was also an extremely vast area.
It was not easy to encounter Evil Sin.
Chen Huangyi had been hiding in the black water for so many years, but he had only encountered it twice.
He was lucky to escape once, and the other time he encountered evil, he was lured away by the demons.
This was the first time Luo Zheng stepped into the Meditation Sea, and he actually encountered evil. His luck was too bad.
"Whoosh …"
In the blink of an eye, the spirals approached Luo Zheng and Chen Huangyi.
The two of them drew their swords at the same time and attacked at the same time!
"Whoosh, whoosh!"
In the black water, any power would be greatly reduced.
When Luo Zheng slashed out with his sword, the sword light became very weak under the suppression of the black water, and the speed at which it flew out became extremely slow!
Luo Zheng frowned as he looked at the sword-light that was slowly spreading.

These little Evil Demons were so agile. Even if the Saints did not use their Divine Consciousness to lock onto them, they would not be able to capture their existence. Jia Lu was helpless against them …
Hearing this, Jia Lu's face was filled with anger, "You damnable fellows, do you want to feed the Ghost Devil Dog?"
Seeing that Jia Lu was angry, the little demons still didn't show any restraint. These guys only feared the strong, and feared the Jia Lu of the past. And the current Jia Lu was simply laughably weak in their eyes.
"Get out of my way!"
Tut, the other Great Demon beside the round table, extended his arm and lightly flicked Garlu's head.
"Bang bang bang bang …"
Those little Abyss Devils shot out like arrows after being flicked by Tut's finger and smashed into the walls of the hall.
Only then did Jia Lu leap up and stand on his own chair.
With his size, he could no longer sit on the huge table and chair. He could only lean on the back of the chair and say to the other demons, "It's your turn!"
When Tut and the other two Great Demons saw Jia Lu's fate, their expressions changed subtly.
In the Abyss Devil Domain, strength was everything.
The winner could take everything they wanted. The four Great Demons stood at the top of the food chain.
Suddenly falling from the top of the pyramid, this psychological drop was indeed uncomfortable …
"Isn't it too high of a price to pay to leave the Abyss Devil Domain?" Tut said lightly, a hint of sadness in his eyes.
"What do you mean? You want to go back on your word?" Jia Lu's face showed a hint of sternness.
If the three Great Demons went back on their word, Jia Lu could do nothing.
Although his identity was still one of the four Great Demons, an identity that had lost its power was just an identity.
Another Great Demon said, "Actually, it's not bad to maintain the old order. At least we have food and drink in the Abyss Devil Domain!"
"But can you stop those Saints?" Jia Lu said angrily.
"We can sit down and talk." Tut chuckled.

Jia Lu smashed his fist on the greenish-grey round table.
However, with his current strength, he could only smash a thin crack on the large round table. He felt a deep sense of powerlessness, and even felt that all the demons had betrayed him.

"Hahaha, Lord Jia Lu is angry …"
"He has such a moment too?"
"Is there any use for the weak to be angry?"
Those little demons clung to the wall and said sarcastically.

These words made Tut and the other Great Demons have a taboo expression.
"We can choose to go back on our word," another Great Demon said calmly.
This Great Demon was called Card Spirit. Its strength was second only to Jia Lu from before, and it was slightly stronger than Tut.

In the Meditation Sea, those unknown things were indeed the overlords, and the demons did not dare to provoke them.
However, outside the Meditation Sea, the demons were the masters of the Abyss Devil Domain!
Hearing the conversation between Card Spirit and Tut, Jia Lu's heart sank …
This meant that its sacrifice would be meaningless, and it also meant that the chair that originally belonged to it on this round table would disappear forever.
"This isn't fair!" Jia Lu's voice was a little hoarse.
"Has there ever been fairness in our race? Hahaha … "
Tut's rhetorical question made Jia Lu speechless. The other Great Demons and the little demons on the wall also laughed shrilly, and the hall was filled with a joyous atmosphere.
However, in the next moment, these laughs suddenly stopped.
A unique feeling spread throughout the hall ….
Even the little demons on the wall became uneasy, and an extreme feeling of fear gripped their hearts.
This feeling only existed in the Meditation Sea, but it actually appeared in the Demon Hall today!
"It's indeed very unfair. Since you've chosen to cooperate with me, how can I give you the chance to go back on your word?"
A cold and indifferent female voice came from the center of the round table.
The smooth surface of the round table began to freeze and frost formed. As the milky white cold air spread, beautiful hexagonal ice crystals were drawn on the table.

Chapter 2427
In the fireplace at the side of the hall, the originally raging flames kept shrinking and quickly extinguishing. Even the ashes quickly became cold.
Some of the demonic spirits behind the demons were shivering.
Tut, Card Spirit, and the other Great Demon's expressions became very ugly …
They were not unfamiliar with this voice.
This was the voice that hid in the Meditation Sea, the voice that taught the demons cultivation method, the voice that allowed the demons to develop and strengthen.

But compared to this body formed by frost, the difference was like the difference between a mortal woman and a fairy.

With its speed, no one would be able to react to its sneak attack!

Every time he took a step closer to the woman, his speed would involuntarily decrease by half. When the little fiend was only two feet away from the woman, he floated in the air as if he was moving in slow motion.
The woman looked at the ugly little fiend and a trace of disgust and contempt appeared on her extremely beautiful face. She only said indifferently, "If the temperature drops to the extreme, all movements will slow down … Since you're not willing to stay outside, then go back!"

Even more brown light rushed out of the Great Demon Demon Palace and hit the bodies of the other Great Demons …

Although these Great Demons were powerful, they had never truly descended into this world. The traces they left behind, their powers, and everything about them were just "imagined".

Tut, Carling, and the other Great Demons were shocked …

Chapter 2428

Ferocious creatures that died would all hand over excess power of faith.
A portion of this power of faith would turn into Shenwu coins, while some would silently nourish the entire Abyss Demon Territory.

The only place that depleted the power of faith was the Shenwu coins that were taken away by the wealthy families. Chapter 2431

A black claw mark pierced through space and arrived in front of the woman.

As if it had been calculated, it directly collided with the black claw mark …
"Puff!"
As the two collided, only a muffled sound was heard, and it was quickly annihilated.

Although Dongfang Chunjun attacked rashly, this woman did not seem to be angered.

"It's with me." The woman smiled slightly.
Hearing this, all the Saints present revealed a hint of emotion.
Could it be that this woman's strength was so strong because she had that faith treasure?
Everyone's eyes were burning with passion, especially those Perfect True Gods. If they had that faith treasure, their strength and status would instantly be on par with Dongfang Chunjun, and they might even surpass him!

"Is that so?" The woman's face showed a hint of anticipation.
Seeing this woman's expression, Dongfang Chunjun was delighted. He did not know where this woman came from, but he still felt that something was not right. Perhaps he could really get that faith treasure from her?
"Yes." Dongfang Chunjun nodded solemnly. "I, Dongfang Chunjun, am the number one Saint. If I can't do it, I'm afraid no one else can!"

The Aristocratic Alliance wanted to unify God's Domain, but they were still a part of God's Domain. No matter how crazy they were, they wouldn't do such a thing.
However, this woman's expression was serious and didn't seem to be faking it …
Dongfang Chunjun's expression froze, and he asked rather helplessly, "What enmity do you have with the people of the divine realm?"
Strictly speaking, all True Gods, including the Saints, belonged to the category of Gods.
Did this mean that this woman had enmity with all the humans in God's Domain?
"Because you don't deserve to own this world. You don't have the right …" The woman suddenly changed the topic halfway through her sentence. "There's no point in saying more. That faith treasure is in my ice palace. If you want to take it away, it depends on whether you have the strength and luck …"

The huge cloud above the Abyss Demonic Realm spun even more violently.
The black cloud had completely covered the sixth and seventh levels of the Abyss Demonic Realm. The entire world seemed to be immersed in eternal night.

As he healed himself, he kept cursing that woman.

Chapter 2432

Luo Zheng's conjecture was the same as Chen Huang Yijian's.

Chapter 2433

Chapter 2434

Those demons and countless ferocious creatures in the Abyss Demon Domain had been forced to "die" by this woman. The faith power that they released was completely collected by her. Chapter 2435

Luo Zheng and Chen Huang Yijian didn't resist too much.
In their eyes, this woman wasn't a good person …
However, this woman was too strong, and the two of them could not escape in the end. Now, they could only act according to the situation.

"Hua …"
The sky and earth were spinning, and the space was shuttling …
"Eh?"
After stepping into the space passage below the ice coffin, Luo Zheng had a strange expression on his face.
From Huan Yu to the Realm of the Gods, Luo Zheng had also experienced all kinds of strange spatial passages. For example, the time passage constructed by the imperial jade seal was also incomparably strange.
But this time, he felt even more wondrous …
He wasn't traveling through the spatial passageway. Instead, he was constantly shrinking!
It was as if he had shrunk back to his normal size from the Aragami that was tens of thousands of feet tall.
However, the shrunken size of the Aragami only lasted for a brief moment. Meanwhile, the feeling that he was experiencing right now was still lingering on. He was not sure if it was just an illusion or not.

There was no reference in this space. Everyone was rapidly shrinking at the same time. Originally, it was very difficult to notice.
Luo Zheng looked at the rotating void around him. He frowned and asked, "How small will we become?"
The woman thought for a moment and then answered, "Probably like dust …"
As soon as she finished speaking, the originally crazily rotating void receded from Luo Zheng's sight …
"Huff … huff … huff …"
A crazy whistling sound roared in Luo Zheng's ears.
Huge pieces of ice crystals drew a curve in the air and smashed towards his head.
"Pa!"
Luo Zheng punched out. The ice crystals that were floating towards him suddenly shattered into countless pieces.
But just as he smashed a piece, more ice crystals came crashing towards him …
At this time, Luo Zheng realized that these huge ice crystals should be just snow!
He had now shrunk countless times. Even the insignificant snow particles looked as big as two or three feet!
"Divine Night Fire Shield!"
"Hua!"
Tang Lun's illusion shouted. A three-colored flame formed a huge fire shield, enveloping the duplicates of the Saints, Luo Zheng, and Chen Huang Yijian.
This three-colored Divine Night Fire was one of Tang Lun's most powerful consummate skills.
After turning into a fire shield, those huge "snow particles" flew over. Just as they approached the fire shield, they turned into a mist!
As for the inside of the Divine Night Fire Shield, it was overflowing with warmth!

"Act according to the circumstances. I don't think she will succeed so easily. Dongfang Chunjun and the others will not give up so easily. Besides, her duplicates may not be stronger than her original body," Luo Zheng said.
In theory, this woman's duplicates were almost perfect, but in reality, there were still many differences.
For example, the strength of Luo Zheng's duplicates was quite different from that of Luo Zheng.
From this point of view, Dongfang Chunjun and the other Saints might not have methods that she could not copy …
"That's the only way," Chen Huang Yijian replied.
The current situation was indeed too complicated.
Whether it was Dongfang Chunjun's side or this woman's side, the two of them were far from being a match. They could only hope that both sides would suffer!
"Huff, huff, huff …"
Everyone descended from the sky. The snowstorm did not show any signs of stopping. Countless "snow particles" smashed onto the Divine Night Fire Shield, evaporating wisps of mist. This mist turned into even finer ice crystals under the extremely low temperature and drifted away with the wind.
After the time it took for an incense stick to burn, everyone finally landed on a snowy plain …
As soon as they landed, a violent roar came from the depths of the snowfield. Then, a shock wave visible to the naked eye spread out.
This shock wave rapidly spread across the snowy plains like an invisible plow plowing through the accumulated snow. Along with this shock wave, there was a bone-chilling cold. However, when this cold attacked, even the "Divine Night Fire Shield" instantly dimmed. Tang Lun's clone's face turned even paler!

Luo Zheng was also shocked. This cold air swept across his body, and all the blood in his body seemed to freeze.
What confused him even more was that this cold air was not produced by the Taoist connotation of the Black Ice Divine Way, but an indescribable power. However, Luo Zheng had never come into contact with this kind of power.

Chapter 2441

The woman's fine eyelashes blinked. She stared at Luo Zheng and said, "Of course. I'm so familiar with this place. My judgment is not far from the truth. I remember that when I tried 8,500 times, using Chen Huang Yijian's clone was enough to deal with the fury of the True Demon."

In this Heavenly Inferno Hell, countless fire creatures were nurtured. Any one of them released was enough to burn a piece of heaven and earth.

However, this quasi-saint's face was still incomparably unsightly.
Outside the Heavenly Inferno Hell, a circle of cold air had already enveloped this Heavenly Inferno Hell. As the white fog shrank, it rapidly invaded the Heavenly Inferno Hell!
After the raging flames in this Heavenly Inferno Hell came into contact with the cold air, they were quickly extinguished. Countless raging fire creatures in the Heavenly Inferno Hell were screaming and roaring. It was as if this cold air was the most fatal poison.
The actual situation was indeed so …
As the Heavenly Inferno Hell continued to shrink, the quasi-saint's hope was also shattered.

Chapter 2443

. "Yes, but its intelligence is only at a very elementary level. It will resist others from possessing it." The woman replied.

Chapter 2444

The grooves on the cave wall were created by the replica of Chen Huangyi's sword that she controlled!

It was simply wishful thinking to rush to the bottom of the ice volcano during this interval. In the end, the woman came up with such a method.
The grooves on the wall could allow people to temporarily hide, which was to dodge back and forth in the grooves to avoid the cold air!

Chapter 2453

If you attack the blue light ball, all the energy will become more active and continue to flow to the red light ball … For countless years, the energy between the two light balls has been constantly changing! If you come here in another year, the ice palace at the bottom of the Abyss Demon Territory will no longer be an ice palace but a fire palace. This ice palace had been maintained for nine years …

Luo Zheng roughly understood. The environment in the ice coffin depended on the two directions of energy flow.
If the energy was more active, it would turn into a sea of fire inside and outside the ice coffin. If the energy was stagnant, it would return to a world of ice and snow.
Now, the energy was gradually becoming more active …
Therefore, the blue light ball was far weaker than the red light ball!

Chapter 2455

At the same time, the red ball of light faintly expanded …

News of the Emperor's death

The other Sages were not only wary of Dongfang Chunjun's strength. If he started a war with the universe, perhaps no Sages in the Divine Realm would dare to accept it.
3,300 races, nine Brahma Realms, thirteen Celestial Stars …
Among the secondary lifeforms, there were countless geniuses. Some of these geniuses were not to be outdone even after stepping into the Divine Realm. However, in the face of the great calamity of the universe's collapse, their eyes were filled with unwillingness and confusion.
Stars fell, great realms collapsed, and countless people died …
This was also the sorrow of the secondary lifeforms.
…
…
In the Waterspout Forest …
Yu Taibai stood in the courtyard, looking up at the sky.
Yu Shenfeng, Jing Wuhuan, Aunt Hanjiu, and the others all looked at him.
"Rumble rumble rumble …"
He crossed his fingers, a thoughtful expression on his face.
Every time there was a rumble, his hand gestures would change, appearing unfathomable.
After a long time …
A bright light flashed in his eyes.
"How is it?" Jing Wuhuan was the first to ask.
When the universe collapsed, Jing Wuhuan and Yu Shenfeng were naturally most concerned about their master, Gu Bei.
Although they did not know where their master was, as long as the universe was safe, at least it meant that their master was still alive.
Yu Taibai's eyes revealed a trace of doubt, as if suspecting that he had miscalculated. He checked with his fingers again, then said, "The sound came from the northwest. The collapsing universe belongs to … Dongfang Chunjun."
"Dongfang Chunjun is dead?" Yu Shenfeng's mouth opened wide.
Jing Wuhuan's eyes also revealed a trace of sharpness. He knew very well what this meant. "Even the universe collapsed. He has no more life!"
The reason why Luo Xiao still had a chance to be reborn was because the Great Expansion Universe and its existence were still operating perfectly.

Chapter 2490
Translator: Henyee Translations Editor: Henyee Translations
Cellar passageway
During this time, Jing Wuhuan and Yu Taibai had been running around.
They knew Dongfang Chunjun's goal in conquering the Abyss Devil Zone.
If Dongfang Chunjun successfully took control of the Abyss Devil Zone, he would probably make a big move after returning to the floating island.
They weren't the only ones who saw this. The other factions also saw it clearly.
Jing Wuhuan relied on this to convince the Xuanyue Clan.
The Xuanyue Clan decided to stand on the same side as the Sword Tribe. If the Sword Tribe decided to go against the Wealthy Alliance, the Xuanyue Clan would also join.
Yu Taibai was trying to find the scattered Daoists in the Divine Dominion.
The Divine Dominion was so big that it wasn't easy to find the Daoists.
Even if Yu Taibai used the Dayan number to deduce, the range was too big. However, it wasn't impossible to find them.
Because the Daoists would trigger the rules of the slate when they sneaked into the Divine Dominion, Yu Taibai could roughly estimate the time and range.
Although the Daoists in the universe were second-level lifeforms, their talent was comparable to the geniuses in the Divine Dominion. These people were all dazzling existences. Given some time, there was bound to be a way to find them!
All the preparations were carried out in an orderly manner. However, they didn't expect Dongfang Chunjun to die so suddenly.
"Dongfang Chunjun is dead. The Wealthy Alliance's conquest of the Abyss Devil Zone failed?" Jing Wuhuan frowned.
"If that's the case, then this is good news." Yu Shenfeng chuckled.
Yu Taibai didn't look too relaxed. He said calmly, "Dongfang Chunjun alone isn't enough to cause us to be afraid …"
Although the Dongfang Clan represented by Dongfang Chunjun was powerful, it wasn't necessarily invincible.
The old monsters in the Sword Tribe and the Xuanyue Clan weren't to be underestimated either. The reason why these two factions hadn't made a move for so many years wasn't just because they were afraid of Dongfang Chunjun, but also because of the Xuanyuan Guard behind him.
"Then what do we do next?" Jing Wuhuan asked.
Yu Taibai thought for a moment and said, "Wait and see …"
Everyone nodded, but there was still a trace of worry in their hearts. That was because of Luo Zheng.
Although the journey to the Evil Ghost Realm was long, and with Luo Zheng's speed, he should have returned by now. However, there was still no news from Luo Zheng.
The Evil Ghost Realm was close to the Abyss Demon Realm, but the entrance to the Abyss Demon Realm was not in the Evil Ghost Realm, so the possibility of Luo Zheng getting involved was not high, but they all had a trace of worry in their hearts.
…
…
At the bottom of the Abyss Demon Domain …
Emperor Hanqing leaped out of the coffin and glared at it fiercely.
Although he did not get the True Demon's body, it would be nice if he could get Dongfang Chunjun's treasure body at the last moment. However, he was still stopped by that damned woman.
Buzz!
He swung the Divine Spear of Tai He, and a powerful spatial fluctuation shot out from the tip of the spear towards the coffin.
Emperor Hanqing didn't expect his spear to destroy the coffin.
After all, this coffin had been here for many years. He had only attacked it to vent his anger.
According to his thoughts, this coffin should be an illusion floating at the entrance of the space. This spear should have pierced through the coffin, but not the coffin itself.
Clang!
At this moment, something unexpected happened.
His shot actually hit the coffin, causing a muffled sound to be emitted …
The sound gave Emperor Hanqing a fright!
His experience on the way here had taught him that Saints were not all that strong. In this damned place, even the slightest bit of carelessness could cost him his life.
The Divine Spear of Tai He pushed the coffin aside.
Below the coffin was a dark entrance to a cellar.
Below the entrance was a neat row of stairs that extended downwards until it was engulfed by darkness. No one knew where it led to.
Emperor Hanqing's heart skipped a beat when he saw the entrance. He realized that he had discovered some incredible secret.
"This coffin's main body floats in the void. Anyone who approaches it will be sucked into the coffin, unable to open it at all. Although that woman has always been here, the Abyssal Infernal Domain suppresses the Spatial Laws, making it impossible for her to open the coffin … but what is hidden in this coffin?
Emperor Hanqing was a cautious person. He stood at the entrance with a trace of hesitation.
Hum!
A blue light suddenly flashed in his eyes, and this blue light entered the darkness of the cellar. It was unknown what it saw in the cellar, but it revealed an expression of interest, which made him make up his mind and step into the cellar.
Stepping on the stairs of the cellar, he stabbed the bottom of the coffin with his spear and dragged the coffin back to its original position.
If that woman left the coffin and found this cellar, she might follow him here. That woman's strength wasn't weaker than his. Moreover, Luo Zheng and Hua Tianming were hard to deal with, so he didn't have to worry about her.
After doing all this, he walked deeper into the cellar.
If Luo Zheng and the others were in the world of ice and snow, they would've felt the entire world shake.
When the coffin was sent flying by Emperor Hanqing, the snow on the snowy mountain began to crumble, forming a violent avalanche.
Unfortunately, they were in the void of the ice volcano and didn't feel the changes outside.
Luo Zheng was still telling his mother about his experience in the outer region of chaos …
He talked about the grudges between the Desolate God, Xuanyuan clan, Nuwa clan, and Chi You clan.
Li Luoshui, the woman, and Chen Huang Yijian all opened their eyes wide.
Chen Huang Yijian also felt that his trip to the divine realm was very strange, but compared to Luo Zheng, it paled in comparison.
"In other words, the Nirvana realm of Nuwa clan and Li clan are in the outer region?" Li Luoshui asked.
Luo Zheng nodded, "But they're blocked by the space barrier and can't come in."
"Elder Jin, five star Chi You guard," Li Luoshui murmured, "I've never heard of this person …"
Lingshu smiled, "Sister wasn't very old when she left the mother world. Our Li clan has many talents. Although the five star Chi You guard is strong, there are still many after all these years. Elder Jin must've left the Li clan a long time ago. It's normal that you haven't heard of him."
Luo Zheng also nodded. He knew that other than Fu Er, the others had never heard of Li Luoshui. His mother hadn't even been born at that time.
"But a five star Chi You guard should be able to deal with Pang Miao," Li Luoshui said blankly.
Her Li clan's mortal enemy wasn't Dongfang Chunjun …
At best, Dongfang Chunjun had released "Huyan" from the seal for his own ambition.

Chapter 2491
Translator: Henyee Translations Editor: Henyee Translations
The change at the entrance
Luo Zheng's news made Li Luoshui and Lingshu's hope increase countless times.
They knew it was difficult for people from the outer realm to come in, but it was only a matter of time.
Within ten years, those Nirvana Realm elites would be able to break through the space barrier. When that time came, they would be able to sweep through everything …
This space was not a place to stay for long.
After Luo Zheng roughly explained what had happened in the outer realm, Lingshu ordered Emperor Hanqing's clone to break through the space.
The frost fiends in the frost fiend nest at the bottom of the ice mountain had been completely reborn.
Lingshu knew everything at the bottom of the ice mountain. The instant she broke through the space, Emperor Hanqing's clone had already created a space tunnel and sent everyone to the top of the ice mountain.
"What happened to the ice mountain?" Chen Huang Yijian looked at the ice mountain with a strange expression.
Lingshu also said strangely, "It seems like there was an avalanche. I've never seen anything as strange as this before."
The snow cover on this large mountain range was shaken off uniformly, revealing the bare mountaintops. The mountaintops were only covered with a thin layer of fresh snow.
Hua Tianming said, "Perhaps the fight in the space caused too much of a commotion and caused the snow to collapse."
"Impossible." Lingshu shook her head.
The space at the bottom of the ice mountain was completely isolated from the outside world. No matter how much of a commotion was caused in the space, it was impossible to cause the snow to collapse.
"Perhaps Emperor Hanqing attacked to vent his anger?" Chen Huang Yijian guessed.
"That's possible." Lingshu nodded.
Without lingering for too long, Ling Shu led the others upwards. Very soon, they left the coffin and returned to the bottom of the huge ice palace.
When Luo Zheng and Hua Tianming left the coffin at the same time, the girl originally lying in the coffin turned into golden dust and disappeared in front of everyone's eyes.
The true demon body had already been taken away by Luo Zheng and Hua Tianming.
Lingshu looked at the girl disappearing from the corner of her eye and showed a trace of disappointment. "I fought for this true demon body for so long, but in the end, it was taken away by you two brats …"
Although Lingshu was a ferocious being, she still had complete memories and emotions.
She had gone in and out of the coffin hundreds of thousands of times. She had even died in it many times, only to be reborn in the Black Water Domain.
Because her own strength was not enough, she went to support the Evil Demon Clan, and also helped Chen Huang Yi Sword … She used all kinds of methods, but it was all in vain. Naturally, she was somewhat disappointed.
Li Luoshui looked at her sister and raised her willowy eyebrows. "If you don't have a True Demon Body, you won't be able to leave the Abyss Devil Domain?"
Lingshu nodded, but shook her head at the same time. "To be precise, I don't really need to rely on my True Demon body to leave the Abyssal Demon Realm. I need to control the Abyssal Demon Realm …"
At this point, Lingshu suddenly realized something.
Chen Huang Yijian also understood at the same time and asked, "Then who is in control of the Abyss Demon Domain now?"
Luo Zheng and Hua Tianming also had strange expressions on their faces …
If the True Demon Body was the Level 6 Xinyang Treasure of the Abyss Devil Domain, they should be in control of the Abyssal Demon Realm now. However, the two girls in their bodies did not move at all.
Luo Zheng had seen Yu Shen Feng control the Takeshi Swirl Forest. Everything in the forest was controlled by Yu Shen Feng. It could be said that Yu Shen Feng was the master of the Takeshi Swirl Forest.
Li Luoshui thought for a while before saying, "Could it be because the True Demon Body was split into two? If so, you can combine the two girls into one and have one person control it. "
Luo Zheng and Hua Tianming looked at each other and nodded slightly.
They did not have a reason to control the True Demon Body. It was the same to give it to anyone. They could even give the two True Demon Bodies to Lingshu.
Then, the two called out the girls in their bodies and tried to separate them from their bodies.
But after experimenting for a while, they were unable to separate …
The two girls were also confused and did not understand what Luo Zheng and Hua Tianming were doing.
This was especially true for the red-eyed girl. Towards the end, she simply snuggled into Luo Zheng's arms. She seemed to have understood what Luo Zheng meant, but no matter what he asked her, she would shake her little head like a rattle-drum, refusing to cooperate.
After a while, the two could only choose to give up.
Although Lingshu was disappointed, she still had hope and said, "It can't be done now, but it doesn't mean it can't be done in the future. If one of you controls the Abyssal Demon Realm in the future, just come back and take me out of the Abyssal Demon Realm!"
After being trapped here for so many years, she dreamed of returning.
"That's the only way. We will think of a way as soon as we go back," Luo Zheng said with a nod.
Then, Lingshu left the Ice Palace with Luo Zheng and the others.
Outside the Ice Palace, on the seventh floor of the Abyssal Demon Realm, the demons were still guarding outside.
These demons were no longer a threat. After being killed once, the power of faith needed to be accumulated again, and the strength of the Demon Race had also fallen to the bottom.
However, even if those demons still had their former strength, under Lingshu's leadership, no one would dare to act rashly.
Under the fearful gazes of the demons, Luo Zheng and the others returned from the seventh floor to the sixth floor.
The Abyssal Demon Realm was huge. Even if there were no obstacles along the way, it would take some time to return to the entrance of the Abyssal Demon Realm.
Three days later …
Lingshu finally sent Luo Zheng and the others to the entrance.
The two sisters talked about many things in the past along the way. They were reluctant to part with each other.
"Lingshu, don't be afraid. Believe in your sister. It won't be long before you leave this place," Li Luoshui consoled.
Lingshu nodded …
Her resentment for so many years was all on the Xuanyuan Guards and the revival of the Li Race.
Now that she knew that her sister was still alive and that the Li Race had someone to shoulder the burden, the resentment in her heart gradually dissipated.
"Luo Zheng, you must think of a way when you go back! Aunty will return to the Black Water Region and wait for your news. "Lingshu smiled at Luo Zheng.
"Zheng 'er will not disappoint Aunty." Luo Zheng waved at Lingshu.
The entrance to the Abyssal Demon Realm was like the mouth of a monster, exuding a fierce aura.
Outside the entrance, there were dozens of teleportation formations. These teleportation formations led directly to the floating island.
In order to monopolize the forbidden areas, the rich and powerful families had set up powerful restrictions at the entrances of the forbidden areas. Everyone could only enter the forbidden areas through the floating island.
"If we return, we can only enter the floating island through the teleportation formation … However, there is another way. That is to break the restrictions of the Abyssal Demon Realm," Hua Tianming said. "With our current strength, it is not difficult to break the restrictions, but it will take some time."
"Return through the floating island," Luo Zheng said calmly.
With all the Saints dead, with the strength of Luo Zheng and the others, there was no one worthy of their fear.
The only one who could be considered a threat was the Xuanyuan Guards. Through Dongfang Chunjun's memories, Luo Zheng also learned that Elder Hu had left the floating island long ago …
Just as they made their decision, an invisible wave of energy suddenly spread out.
"Crack … crack …"
The ground in front of the Abyssal Demon Realm suddenly cracked.
The soil in the cracked ground turned into a head that was tens of thousands of feet tall. It rolled up a cloud of dust and opened its bloody mouth to swallow everyone.
